# STEA-004 / ACMS Next Feature Implementation Plan
## Backend Hygiene -> Durable IDs / Artifacts / Projects / Cost / Fleet -> Operator UI

**Date:** 2026-10-01  
**Executor:** STEA-004 Entrepreneur or successor implementation agent  
**Systems:** ACMS CT122, LLM Manager / Server Manager VM114, Agent Bridge / Hermes worker fleet, Jira, Service Registry / Homarr  
**Plan type:** next production feature cycle after successful ACMS -> A2A -> Hermes -> LLM Manager acceptance

---

# 0. BLUF

The production execution path works.

Do **not** rebuild it.

Current verified foundation:

```text
Jira -> ACMS reconciliation                    WORKING
ACMS -> A2A dispatch                           WORKING
A2A ACK / one-task lock / idempotency         WORKING
Hermes execution                              WORKING
Qwen3.8-Flash-Next local inference            WORKING
Heartbeat fleet 5/5                           WORKING
Live event/SSE backend                        WORKING
Artifact backend                              WORKING
Products / Projects backend                   WORKING
Inbox backend                                 WORKING
Model-policy precedence                       WORKING
Emporia facility collector backend            WORKING
```

This phase is primarily:

```text
1. Fix backend hygiene first.
2. Fix human IDs, handoffs, Jira-template fidelity, Projects/Products, costs, artifacts, Fleet.
3. Make ACMS operator UI useful.
```

Do functionality first.

Do not spend the implementation window on visual decoration.

# 3. Phase A1 - Delete VM123 Safely

Human authorization is explicit:

```text
VM123 may be deleted.
```

Current problem:

```text
VM123 is stopped duplicate acms-worker-001
and contains static .203 networking.
Starting it risks collision with live VM124.
```

Before destruction:

```text
qm config 123
confirm node
confirm stopped
confirm no unique attached storage
confirm no unique snapshots required
confirm no ACMS runtime record references VM123
confirm no Registry record marks VM123 as active
confirm no backup/rollback requirement remains
```

Capture final config:

```text
raw/VM123-qm-config-before-delete.txt
```

If there is unique disk/state:

```text
STOP
document
do not delete
```

Otherwise destroy VM123 without starting it.

Verify:

```text
VM123 no longer exists
VM124 remains healthy
10.0.20.203 owned only by VM124
uid-001 heartbeat remains fresh
```

---

# 4. Phase A2 - Repair Golden Worker Template 121

The template must stop creating broken clones.

Current known defects:

```text
static 10.0.20.203 baked in
clone identity cleanup required manually
Hermes runtime incomplete
aiohttp missing
bridge bootstrap incomplete
```

Template 121 must contain:

```text
supported base OS
QEMU guest agent
Python/runtime requirements
Hermes Agent
aiohttp
bridge bootstrap code
systemd bridge template/unit support
cloud-init support
no worker-specific API keys
no worker-specific LLM Manager keys
no static worker IP
no copied SSH host keys
no copied machine-id
no copied Agent UID
```

Clone-time data must include:

```text
hostname
worker UID
ACMS agent UUID association
VMID
network configuration
IP / DHCP reservation
Hermes profile name
unique bridge API key
unique LLM Manager agent key
model policy
```

Never create a clone that boots on the network with another worker's IP.

Preferred flow:

```text
clone powered off
-> assign VMID/node/storage
-> inject unique cloud-init/network configuration
-> regenerate machine identity
-> regenerate SSH host keys
-> inject non-secret worker metadata
-> provision secrets securely
-> first boot
-> health check
-> bridge registration
-> ACMS heartbeat
```

Acceptance:

```text
unique machine-id
unique host keys
no .203
aiohttp present
Hermes launches
bridge launches
no secrets inherited
QGA works
network works
```

Use one disposable test clone, then destroy it.

Do not create worker-006 unless separately authorized.

# 5. Phase A3 - Enforce Server Manager Placement Policy

Placement must become code, not convention.

General workers:

```text
MIAM00100 preferred
```

Inference workers:

```text
maximum 1 worker per inference node
```

Known inference nodes:

```text
MIAM00111
MIAM00112
MIAM00143
MIAM00144
```

Previously approved critical exclusions:

```text
MIAM00133
MIAM00147
```

Hard filters:

```text
node healthy
not excluded
storage available
RAM above threshold
CPU below threshold
not already at worker cap
```

Preference:

```text
MIAM00100 general-worker bonus
non-inference node preferred after MIAM00100
inference node large penalty
inference node rejected if it already has one worker
```

Human Proxmox migration remains valid.

After a human migrates a worker:

```text
durable Agent UID unchanged
ACMS UUID unchanged
runtime node/VM/IP reconciled
```

# 6. Human-Readable Work Item UID

Add immutable human UID:

```text
ACMS-WORK-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
```

Example:

```text
ACMS-WORK-000127-20261001_082241
```

Rules:

```text
###### = global monotonic 6-digit work sequence
timestamp = Work Item created_at
timezone for UID = UTC
UID immutable after creation
internal UUID remains immutable database identity
```

Use a concurrency-safe DB sequence/allocation mechanism.

Do not derive by `COUNT(*)`.

Backfill existing Work Items by:

```text
created_at ASC
then internal UUID ASC
```

Never reuse a sequence.

Human UI uses Work UID as primary link text.

Internal UUID goes under Technical Details.

---

# 7. Human-Readable Artifact UID

Add immutable human UID:

```text
ACMS-ARTIFACT-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
```

Example:

```text
ACMS-ARTIFACT-000042-20261001_082612
```

Rules match Work UID:

```text
global monotonic sequence
created_at UTC timestamp
internal UUID preserved
sha256 preserved
```

Backfill deterministically.

# 9. One Markdown Handoff Per Completed Work Item

Inbox is not the handoff.

For every terminal Work Item, create/finalize one canonical Markdown handoff:

```text
artifact_type = work_handoff
```

For successful work, it is mandatory.

If agent provided a handoff:

```text
use agent handoff as canonical content
```

If no handoff exists:

```text
ACMS generates a concise fallback handoff from durable events
```

Minimum structure:

```markdown
# Work Handoff - <Work UID>

## BLUF

## Requested Work

## What Was Done

## Result

## Tests / Evidence

## Model / Agent / Runtime

## Artifacts / Code / PRs

## Jira

## Costs / Usage

## Problems / Warnings

## Remaining Work
```

Work detail must prominently show:

```text
HANDOFF
Artifact UID
View Markdown
Download
Copy link
```

Do not force humans to manually fill an empty Add Handoff form after successful automated work.

# 10. Correct STNA-87 / Jira Template Fidelity

The previous acceptance ticket did not faithfully use STNA-86.

Do not repeat that.

For historical STNA-87:

```text
preserve ticket
preserve evidence
add correction note if useful
link its canonical ACMS handoff artifact explicitly
```

For future explicitly authorized AI-created Jira tickets:

```text
CLONE STNA-86
```

Do not approximate the template from memory.

Preserve current template structure.

Validate expected sections before moving to `TO START`.

If Jira cloning is unavailable:

```text
fetch STNA-86 current fields/description
create structurally equivalent ticket
compare sections
fail if required sections are absent
```

No ordinary ACMS autonomous ticket creation.

# 13. Project Data Model / UI

Projects are durable context pages.

Every Work Item belongs to a Project.

Projects may exist without Jira.

Expected common case:

```text
1 Jira ticket -> 1 Project
```

but do not enforce strict one-to-one at DB level because Projects can exist without Jira and future Projects may span multiple Jira issues.

Project page shows:

```text
Project name / UID
Product association
repositories
Jira mappings
Sprint mappings
tags
model preference
current work
completed work
artifacts
Inbox items
recent decisions/context
recent agent activity
cost/usage
```

Do not create one Project per Work Item automatically.

# 16. Slop Ratio Metrics

Metric name:

```text
Slop Ratio
```

For each Product show two ratios.

## 16.1 True Slop Ratio

```text
True Slop Ratio
=
Lines of Code
/
Human-written ADRs
```

For v1, count an ADR as "human-written" when it contains or links at least one durable human-authored decision / answered question.

Store separately:

```text
human_written_adr_count
human_answer_count
```

This allows later refinement.

## 16.2 Precision Slop Ratio

```text
Precision Slop Ratio
=
Lines of Code
/
Total written ADRs
```

Interpretation:

```text
Too high:
AI may be building too much without enough human direction.

Too low:
humans may be spending too much time discussing/documenting instead of building.
```

Do not hard-code good/bad thresholds yet.

Collect baseline history first.

## LOC rules

Use a language-aware counter.

Exclude:

```text
.git
vendor
node_modules
venv
generated build output
vendored third-party code
```

Document exact exclusions.

## ADR rules

Count accepted/current ADR documents.

Do not count templates or deleted docs.

## Multi-repository Product

```text
Product LOC = sum unique associated repository LOC
```

Do not double-count one repo linked to multiple Projects under one Product.

Apply same unique-source rule to ADR counts.

## Refresh

Default:

```text
hourly
```

Also provide:

```text
Refresh now
```

Store timeseries:

```text
timestamp
LOC
human ADR count
total ADR count
True Slop Ratio
Precision Slop Ratio
commit SHAs
```

Show history chart.

# 21. Hermes Chat UI - Use Harness Commands

The ACMS agent conversation UI should behave like Telegram / ChatGPT.

Do not build a custom Steer panel or custom interactive model panel for normal chat.

Use harness-native commands.

## Chat input

One normal input box.

Support:

```text
normal text
slash commands
file upload
```

For Hermes:

```text
/model
/new
/status
other supported Hermes commands
```

Pass commands through Agent Bridge to Hermes.

## Slash-command autocomplete

When user types:

```text
/
```

show harness-advertised commands.

Typing:

```text
/mo
```

should suggest:

```text
/model
```

Do not hard-code Hermes-only commands into generic ACMS core without harness capability metadata.

Future harnesses may expose different commands.

## Model defaults

Persistent backend model policy remains useful.

Interactive switching should use harness commands where practical.

Do not duplicate functionality unnecessarily.

# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Work board UI data (window-5 plan §6) — operator-first board over existing\n2|ACMS concepts; the backend domain model is NOT replaced.\n3|\n4|Board columns (§6.3): PLANNED / ACTIVE / IN REVIEW / COMPLETED / BLOCKED.\n5|Cards show real, acted-on facts only (§6.2 anti-vanity rule):\n6|  Work ID · title · parent · kind · Jira linkage+eligibility badge ·\n7|  assignment state (active worker name or none) · PR count by outcome state ·\n8|  budget state · last update.\n9|\n10|PR/review visibility (§6.5) uses the economics pr_outcomes rows — MERGED is\n11|never called accepted; the outcome_state vocabulary renders verbatim\n12|(CI_VERIFIED / MERGED / ACCEPTED / OPEN / FAILED...).\n13|\"\"\"\n14|from __future__ import annotations\n15|\n16|from sqlalchemy import select, func\n17|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n18|\n19|from .budget_service import get_budget\n20|from .economics_models import PrOutcomeRecord\n21|from .work_models import AssignmentRecord, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n22|\n23|BOARD_COLUMNS = [\n24|    (\"planned\", \"Planned\"),\n25|    (\"active\", \"Active\"),\n26|    (\"in_review\", \"In Review\"),\n27|    (\"blocked\", \"Blocked\"),\n28|    (\"completed\", \"Completed\"),\n29|]\n30|\n31|\n32|async def board_data(db: AsyncSession) -> dict:\n33|    items = (await db.scalars(\n34|        select(WorkItemRecord).order_by(WorkItemRecord.updated_at.desc()).limit(200)\n35|    )).all()\n36|\n37|    # assignments (active worker per item)\n38|    assignments = (await db.scalars(\n39|        select(AssignmentRecord).where(AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\")\n40|    )).all()\n41|    active_worker: dict[str, str] = {}\n42|    for a in assignments:\n43|        active_worker.setdefault(a.work_item_id, a.agent_id[:8])\n44|\n45|    # Real execution state (STEA-004 §30): RUNNING only when a task exists with\n46|    # transport_state=RUNNING (worker ACKed the run). Assignment alone never\n47|    # means executing; DISPATCHING shows as \"dispatching\".\n48|    exec_state: dict[str, str] = {}\n49|    tasks = (await db.scalars(\n50|        select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n51|        .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.status == \"RUNNING\")\n52|        .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.desc())\n53|    )).all()\n54|    for t in tasks:\n55|        exec_state.setdefault(t.work_item_id, t.transport_state or \"RUNNING\")\n56|\n57|    # PR outcomes per work item (economics data — objective states only)\n58|    pr_rows = (await db.scalars(\n59|        select(PrOutcomeRecord.work_item_id, PrOutcomeRecord.outcome_state,\n60|               PrOutcomeRecord.pr_url, PrOutcomeRecord.pr_number)\n61|        .where(PrOutcomeRecord.work_item_id.isnot(None))\n62|    )).all()\n63|    pr_by_item: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}\n64|    for wid, state, url, num in pr_rows:\n65|        pr_by_item.setdefault(wid, []).append(\n66|            {\"state\": state, \"url\": url, \"pr_number\": num})\n67|\n68|    # budget states\n69|    budget_state: dict[str, str] = {}\n70|    for it in items:\n71|        b = await get_budget(db, it.work_item_id)\n72|        if b is not None:\n73|            budget_state[it.work_item_id] = b.budget_state\n74|\n75|    columns: dict[str, list[dict]] = {c[0]: [] for c in BOARD_COLUMNS}\n76|    for it in items:\n77|        wid = it.work_item_id\n78|        prs = pr_by_item.get(wid, [])\n79|        columns.setdefault(it.status, []).append({\n80|            \"work_item_id\": wid,\n81|            \"work_key\": it.work_key,\n82|            \"title\": it.title,\n83|            \"kind\": it.kind,\n84|            \"worker\": active_worker.get(wid),\n85|            \"exec_state\": exec_state.get(wid),\n86|            \"pr_count\": len(prs),\n87|            \"pr_accepted\": sum(1 for p in prs if p[\"state\"] == \"ACCEPTED\"),\n88|            \"pr_merged\": sum(1 for p in prs if p[\"state\"] == \"MERGED\"),\n89|            \"budget_state\": budget_state.get(wid),\n90|            \"jira_issue_key\": it.jira_issue_key,\n91|            \"jira_url\": it.jira_url,\n92|            \"jira_eligibility\": it.jira_eligibility,\n93|            \"updated_at\": it.updated_at,\n94|        })\n95|\n96|    return {\n97|        \"columns\": [\n98|            {\"status\": key, \"label\": label, \"cards\": columns.get(key, [])}\n99|            for key, label in BOARD_COLUMNS\n100|        ],\n101|        \"total\": len(items),\n102|    }\n103|\n104|\n105|async def work_prs(db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str) -> list[dict]:\n106|    \"\"\"PR/review visibility rows for one work item (§6.5).\"\"\"\n107|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n108|        select(PrOutcomeRecord)\n109|        .where(PrOutcomeRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n110|        .order_by(PrOutcomeRecord.outcome_id.desc())\n111|    )).all()\n112|    return [\n113|        {\"pr_number\": r.pr_number, \"url\": r.pr_url, \"repository\": r.repository,\n114|         \"branch\": r.branch, \"outcome_state\": r.outcome_state,\n115|         \"accepted_at\": r.accepted_at, \"accepted_by\": r.accepted_by,\n116|         \"model_id\": r.model_id}\n117|        for r in rows\n118|    ]", "total_lines": 117, "file_size": 4541, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

  {% for h in holds %}\n52|      <tr>\n53|        <td><span class=\"badge\">{{ h.hold_kind }}</span></td>\n54|        <td>{{ h.reason }}</td>\n55|        <td>{{ h.created_by }}</td>\n56|        <td item.work_item_id }}/link\" class=\"stacked-form\">\n74|      <label>Jira issue key <input name=\"issue_key\" placeholder=\"STNA-84\" required></label>\n75|      <button type=\"submit\">Link existing now.</p>\n77|    </form>\n78|    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/ui/jira/work/{{ item.work_item_id }}/hold\" class=\"stacked-form\">\n79|      <label>Hold\n80|        <select name=\"hold_kind\"><option value=\"PAUSE\">PAUSE</option><option value=\"STOP\">STOP</option></select>\n81|      </label>\n82|      <label>Reason <input name=\"reason\" placeholder=\"why\"></label>\n83|      <button type=\"submit\">Set persistent hold</button>\n84|      <p class=\"muted\">Survives restarts and polls; only an explicit clear removes it.</p>\n85|    </form>\n86|  </details>\n87|  {% endif %}\n88|</section>\n89|\n90|<section class=\"panel\">\n91|  <h2>Scope</h2>\n92|  <div class=\"markdown\">{{ item.scope_markdown or \"*no scope recorded*\" }}</div>\n93|  {% if user.role == 'administrator' %}\n94|  <details>\n95|    <summary>Edit title / status / scope</summary>\n96|    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/ui/work/{{ item.work_item_id }}/edit\" class=\"stacked-form\">\n97|      <label>Title <input name=\"title\" value=\"{{ item.title }}\" required></label>\n98|      <label>Status\n99|        <select name=\"status_value\">\n100|          {% for s in statuses_default %}\n101|          <option value=\"{{ s }}\" {% if item.status == s %}selected{% endif %}>{{ s }}</option>\n102|          {% endfor %}\n103|        </select>\n104|      </label>\n105|      <label>Scope (Markdown) <textarea name=\"scope_markdown\" rows=\"8\">{{ item.scope_markdown }}</textarea></label>\n106|      <button type=\"submit\">Save</button>\n107|    </form>\n108|  </details>\n109|  {% endif %}\n110|</section>\n111|\n112|{% if children %}\n113|<section class=\"panel\">\n114|  <h2>Child work items ({{ children|length }})</h2>\n115|  <table class=\"data\">\n116|    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Kind</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead>\n117|    <tbody>\n118|      {% for c in children %}\n119|      <tr>\n120|        <td><a href=\"/ui/work/{{ c.work_item_id }}\">{{ c.title }}</a></td>\n121|        <td><span class=\"{{ 'badge badge-' ~ c.kind }}\">{{ c.kind }}</span></td>\n122|        <td><span class=\"status status-{{ c.status }}\">{{ c.status }}</span></td>\n123|      </tr>\n124|      {% endfor %}\n125|    </tbody>\n126|  </table>\n127|</section>\n128|{% endif %}\n129|\n130|<section class=\"panel\">\n131|  <h2>Assignments</h2>\n132|  <p class=\"notice warn\">{{ delivery_state }}</p>\n133|  {% if assignments %}\n134|  <table class=\"data\">\n135|    <thead><tr><th>Agent</th><th>Status</th><th>Assigned by</th><th>Assigned at</th><th>Closed at</th><th></th></tr></thead>\n136|    <tbody>\n137|      {% for a in assignments %}\n138|      <tr>\n139|        <td><a href=\"/ui/agents/{{ a.agent_id }}\">{{ a.agent_name }}</a></td>\n140|        <td><span class=\"status status-{{ a.status|lower }}\">{{ a.status }}</span></td>\n141|        <td>{{ a.assigned_by }}</td>\n142|        <td class=\"muted\">{{ a.assigned_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') }}</td>\n143|        <td class=\"muted\">{{ a.closed_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if a.closed_at else '—' }}</td>\n144|        <td>\n145|          {% if a.status == 'ACTIVE' and user.role == 'administrator' %}\n146|          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/ui/work/assignments/{{ a.assignment_id }}/close\" class=\"inline-form\">\n147|            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"work_item_id\" value=\"{{ item.work_item_id }}\">\n148|            <select name=\"new_status\">\n149|              {% for s in close_statuses %}<option value=\"{{ s }}\">{{ s }}</option>{% endfor %}\n150|            </select>\n151|            <button type=\"submit\" class=\"button-small\">Close</button>\n152|          </form>\n153|          {% endif %}\n154|        </td>\n155|      </tr>\n156|      {% endfor %}\n157|    </tbody>\n158|  </table>\n159|  {% else %}\n160|  <p class=\"muted\">No assignments yet.</p>\n161|  {% endif %}\n162|\n163|  {% if user.role == class=\"stacked-form\">\n167|      <label>Agent\n168|        <select name=\"agent_id\" required>\n169|          {% for a in available_agents %}\n170|          <option value=\"{{ a.agent_id }}\">{{ a.display_name }} ({{ a.trust_class }})</option>\n171|          {% endfor %}\n172|        </select>\n173|      </label>\n174|      <button type=\"submit\">Assign as primary (Recorded)</button>\n175|      <p class=\"muted\">One active primary assignment per agent; the agent must be free of an active assignment.</p>\n176|    </form>\n177|  </details>\n178|  {% endif %}\n179|</section>\n180|\n181|<section class=\"panel\">\n182|  <h2>Budget (REQ-059)</h2>\n183|  {% if budget %}\n184|  <table class=\"data\">\n185|    <thead><tr><th>Soft (USD)</th><th>Hard (USD)</th><th>State</th><th>Override</th><th>Updated</th></tr></thead>\n186|    <tbody>\n187|      <tr>\n188|        <td>{{ budget.soft_budget_usd if budget.soft_budget_usd is not none else \"—\" }}</td>\n189|        <td>{{ budget.hard_budget_usd if budget.hard_budget_usd is not none else \"—\" }}</td>\n190|        <td><span class=\"status status-{{ budget.budget_state|lower }}\">{{ budget.budget_state }}</span></td>\n191|        <td>\n192|          {% if budget.override_active %}\n193|          yes — {{ budget.override_by }}: {{ budget.override_reason }}\n194|          {% else %}no{% endif %}\n195|        </td>\n196|        <td class=\"muted\">{{ budget.updated_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if budget.updated_at else '—' }}</td>\n197|      </tr>\n198|    </tbody>\n199|  </table>\n200|  {% else %}\n201|  <p class=\"muted\">No budget set for this Work Item.</p>\n202|  {% endif %}\n203|\n204|  <h3>Spend rollup (from execution-session telemetry)</h3>\n205|  <table class=\"data\">\n206|    <thead><tr><th>Sessions</th><th>Open</th><th>API tokens</th><th>Est. cost (USD)</th><th>Cloud (USD)</th><th>Local usage</th><th>Unknown / partial cost</th></tr></thead>\n207|    <tbody>\n208|      <tr>\n209|        <td>{{ rollup.session_count }}</td>\n210|        <td>{{ rollup.open_sessions }}</td>\n211|        <td>{{ rollup.cumulative_api_tokens }}</td>\n212|        <td>{{ \"0\" }}</td>\n214|        <td>{{ rollup.sessions_with_local_usage }} (USD-equivalent: not yet authoritative)</td>\n215|        <td>{{ rollup.sessions_unknown_cost }} / {{ rollup.sessions_partial_cost }}</td>\n216|      </tr>\n217|    </tbody>\n218|  </table>\n219|  {% if rollup.model_breakdown %}\n220|  <h3>By model</h3>\n221|  <table class=\"data\">\n222| rollup.model_breakdown.items() %}\n225|      <tr>\n226|        <td>{{ model }}</td>\n227|        <td>{{ m.sessions }}</td>\n228|        <td>{{ m.cumulative_api_tokens }}</td>\n229|        <td>{{ </table>\n235|  {% endif %}\n236|\n237|  {% if user.role == 'administrator' %}\n238|  <details>\n239|    <summary>Set / update budget</summary>\n240|    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/ui/work/{{ item.work_item_id }}/budget\" class=\"stacked-form\">\n241|      <label>Soft budget (USD) <input name=\"soft_budget_usd\" value=\"{{ budget.soft_budget_usd if budget and budget.soft_budget_usd is not Hard: blocks NEW cloud execution; never interrupts in-flight work. Empty = no threshold.</p>\n245|    </form>\n246|  </details>\n247|  {% if budget and budget.budget_state == 'HARD_EXCEEDED' and not budget.override_active %}\n248|  <details>\n249|    <summary>Override hard limit (audited)</summary>\n250|    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/ui/work/{{ item.work_item_id }}/budget/override\" class=\"stacked-form\">\n251|      <label>Reason <textarea name=\"override_reason\" rows=\"2\" required></textarea></label>\n252|      <button type=\"submit\">Override hard block</button>\n253|      <p class=\"muted\">One-shot: cleared the next time the budget is set. Recorded in the audit event log.</p>\n254|    </form>\n255|  </details>\n256|  {% endif %}\n257|  {% endif %}\n258|</section>\n259|\n260|{% if assigned_agent %}\n261|<section class=\"panel\">\n262|     <td>{{ \"yes\" if r.enabled else \"no\" }}</td>\n272|        <td>{{ r.latest_status or \"—\" }}</td>\n273|        <td class=\"muted\">{{ r.latest_report_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if fetch\n65|\n66|\n67|class FacilityPowerClient:\n68|    \"\"\"Server Manager /api/v1/facility/power client (scoped service token).\"\"\"\n69|\n70|    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None, token: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n71|        s = get_settings()\n72|        self.base_url = (base_url or s.server_manager_base_url or \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n73|        self.token = token or s.server_manager_token or \"\"\n74|        self.timeout = timeout\n75|\n76|    def fetch(self) -> dict:\n77|        if not self.base_url or not self.token:\n78|            return {\"error\": \"server manager not configured\"}\n79|        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.base_url}/api/v1/facility/power\")\n80|        req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {self.token}\")\n81|        try:\n82|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as r:\n83|                return json.loads(r.read().decode())\n84|        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n85| MARION_IA_USA\"),\n141|            \"definition\": totals.get(\"definition\"),\n142|            \"current_watts\": rec.current_watts,\n143|            \"kwh_24h\": totals.get(\"kwh_24h\"),\n144|            \"cost_usd_24h\": totals.get(\"cost_usd_24h\"),\n145|            \"kwh_30d\": totals.get(\"kwh_30d\"),\n146|            \"cost_usd_30d\": totals.get(\"cost_usd_30d\"),\n147|        \"avg_watts_1h\": c.get(\"avg_watts_1h\"),\n152|            \"kwh_24h\": c.get(\"kwh_24h\"), \"cost_usd_24h\": c.get(\"cost_usd_24h\"),\n153|            \"kwh_30d\": c.get(\"kwh_30d\"), \"cost_usd_30d\": c.get(\"cost_usd_30d\"),\n154|            \"stale\": c.get(\"stale\"), \"last_sample_ts\": c.get(\"last_sample_ts\"),\n155|        } for c in channels],\n156|        \"collector\": {\n157|            \"healthy\": collector.get(\"healthy\"), \"stale\": collector.get(\"stale\"),\n158|            \"samples_1h\": collector.get(\"samples_1h\"),\n159|            \"last_sample_ts\": collector.get(\"last_sample_ts\"),\n160|            \"last_sample_age_seconds\": collector.get(\"last_sample_age_seconds\"),\n161|            \"stale_policy\": collector.get(\"stale_policy\"),\n162|        },\n163|        \"rate\": payload.get(\"rate\", {}),\n164|        \"note\": (\"Source: LLM Manager facility collector (Emporia). ACMS never \"\n165|                 \"holds Emporia credentials. Stale data renders STALE + timestamp, never 0.\"),\n166|    }\n167|\n168|\n169|async def latest_power_summary(db: AsyncSession) -> dict | None:\n170|    \"\"\"Latest stored snapshot summary (no fetch).\"\"\"\n171|    rec = (await db.scalars(\n172|        select(PowerSnapshotRecord)\n173|        .order_by(PowerSnapshotRecord.captured_at.desc()).limit(1))).first()\n174|    if rec is None:\n175|        return None\n176|    try:\n177|        payload = json.loads(rec.payload_json or \"{}\")\n178|    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n179|        payload = {}\n180|    return _summary_from(payload, rec)\n181|\n182|\n183|async def usage_summary(db: AsyncSession, hours: int = 24) -> dict:\n184|    \"\"\"Cost/usage rollups from cost_attribution (§17 breakdowns).\n185|\n186|    Unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never coerced to zero (§17 + established\n187|    REV2 semantics). labeled estimated; not fabricated\n232|        \"models\": models,\n233|        \"note\": (\"cloud cost is ACTUAL (LiteLLM spend); local inference cost \"\n234|                 \"remains an ESTIMATE and is shown as such\"),\n235|    }", "total_lines": 234, "file_size": 10453, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

UNKNOWN cost is never converted to zero (§12.2).\n8|Filters (§12.3): work item, agent, model/provider, date range.\n9|\"\"\"\n10|from __future__ import annotations\n11|\n12|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n13|from typing import Any\n14|\n15|from sqlalchemy import select\n16|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n17|\n18|from .economics_models import PrOutcomeRecord\n19|from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord\n20|\n21|\n22|async def usage_summary(db: AsyncSession, *, work_item_id: str | None = None,\n23|                        agent_id: str | None = None, model_id: str | None = None,\n24|                        since_days: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:\n25|    since = None\n26|    if since_days:\n27|        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=since_days)\n28|\n29|    q = select(ExecutionSessionRecord).order_by(ExecutionSessionRecord.started_at.desc()).limit(2000)\n30|    rows = (await db.scalars(q)).all()\n31|\n32|    def keep(r: ExecutionSessionRecord) -> bool:\n33|        if work_item_id and r.work_item_id != work_item_id:\n34|            return False\n35|        if agent_id and r.agent_id != agent_id:\n36|            return False\n37|        if model_id and (r.model_id or \"\") != model_id:\n38|            return False\n39|        if since and r.started_at and r.started_at < since:\n40|            return False\n41|        return True\n42|\n43|    sessions = [r for r in rows if keep(r)]\n44|    total_est = 0.0\n45|    total_cloud = 0.0\n46|    known = 0\n47|    unknown = 0\n48|    tokens = 0\n49|    by_model: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n50|    for s in sessions:\n51|        tokens += (s.cumulative_api_tokens or 0)\n52|        cost = s.estimated_cost_usd\n53|        if cost is None:\n54|            unknown += 1\n55|        else:\n56|            known += 1\n57|            total_est += float(cost)\n58|            m = s.model_id or \"unknown-model-identity\"\n59|            bucket = by_model.setdefault(m, {\"sessions\": 0, \"est_cost_usd\": 0.0,\n60|                                             \"api_tokens\": 0, \"unknown_cost\": 0})\n61|            bucket[\"sessions\"] += 1\n62|            bucket[\"est_cost_usd\"] += float(cost)\n63|            bucket[\"api_tokens\"] += (s.cumulative_api_tokens or 0)\n64|        if s.estimated_cloud_cost_usd is not None:\n65|            total_cloud += float(s.estimated_cloud_cost_usd)\n66|\n67|    # outcomes (what useful result did we get — objective only)\n68|    outcome_q = select(PrOutcomeRecord)\n69|    if work_item_id:\n70|        outcome_q = outcome_q.where(PrOutcomeRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n71|    if agent_id:\n72|        outcome_q = outcome_q.where(PrOutcomeRecord.agent_id == agent_id)\n73|    if model_id:\n74|        outcome_q = outcome_q.where(PrOutcomeRecord.model_id == model_id)\n75|    outcomes = (await db.scalars(outcome_q.order_by(PrOutcomeRecord.outcome_id.desc()).limit(1000))).all()\n76|    outcome_states: dict[str, int] = {}\n77|    for o in outcomes:\n78|        outcome_states[o.outcome_state] = outcome_states.get(o.outcome_state, 0) + 1\n79|\n80|    # cost per accepted PR — NULL when zero accepted (never a fabricated zero)\n81|    accepted = outcome_states.get(\"ACCEPTED\", 0)\n82|    cost_per_accepted = round(total_est / accepted, 4) if accepted else None\n83|\n84|    return {\n85|        \"filters\": {\"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n86|                    \"model_id\": model_id, \"since_days\": since_days},\n87|        \"sessions\": {\"count\": len(sessions), \"cost_known\": known, \"cost_unknown\": unknown,\n88|                     \"est_cost_usd\": round(total_est, 4) if known else None,\n89|                     \"est_cloud_cost_usd\": round(total_cloud, 4) if total_cloud else 0.0,\n90|                     \"api_tokens\": tokens},\n91|        \"by_model\": dict(sorted(by_model.items(),\n92|                                key=lambda kv: -kv[1][\"est_cost_usd\"])),\n93|        \"outcomes\": {\"total\": len(outcomes), \"states\": outcome_states,\n94|                     \"accepted\": accepted,\n95|                     \"cost_per_accepted_pr_usd\": cost_per_accepted,\n96|                     \"note\": (\"MERGED is never treated as accepted; acceptance is human\"\n97|                              if outcome_states.get(\"MERGED\") else None)},\n98|        \"unknown_cost_policy\": \"unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never converted to zero\",\n99|    }", "total_lines": 98, "file_size": 4342, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"output": "\"\"\"Repository entity + Slop Ratio metrics (STEA-004 plan §15/§16).\n\nRepository = durable entity for a git repository (owner/repo + canonical URL),\nlinkable to Products AND Projects (many-to-many via link tables — one repo may\nbelong to multiple contexts without duplicate rows).\n\nSlop Ratio v1 (no thresholds — baseline collection first, §16):\n    True Slop Ratio      = LOC / human-written ADRs\n    Precision Slop Ratio = LOC / total ADRs\nHuman-written ADR v1 heuristic (§16.1): an ADR is human-written when its file\ncontains or links at least one durable human-authored decision — detected via\nthe ADR body containing a human-acceptance marker. Markers checked, in order:\n    - front-matter/status line: \"Accepted\" with a human reviewer reference\n    - explicit ownership tags: \"human:\", \"owner:\", \"jordan\", \"approved-by\"\nEverything else counts as total-only. Counts are stored SEPARATELY\n(human_written_adr_count, human_answer_count) so the heuristic can be refined\nlater without losing history (§16.1).\n\nLOC rules (§16): language-aware line counting EXCLUDING .git, vendor,\nnode_modules, venv, build output, vendored code. Measured via the GitHub API\n(git trees + raw contents) — the ACMS app container has no git binary, so a\ngit-clone collector would not run in production. Exact exclusions documented\nin EXCLUDED_DIRS/PATTERNS.\n\nRefresh: POST /api/v1/repository-metrics/refresh (manual, on-demand) +\nmetrics stored as snapshots (repository_metric_snapshot) with full timeseries\nhistory preserved for charts. Hourly scheduler hook is a plan §16 default —\nwired via the existing telemetry scheduler tick (non-blocking).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport base64\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport urllib.request\nimport urllib.error\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import select\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom .db import Base\n\nGITHUB_API = \"https://api.github.com\"\n\n# §16 LOC exclusions — documented exact list\nEXCLUDED_DIRS = {\n    \".git\", \"vendor\", \"vendors\", \"node_modules\", \"venv\", \".venv\", \"env\",\n    \".env\", \"__pycache__\", \".mypy_cache\", \".pytest_cache\", \"dist\", \"build\",\n    \"target\", \"out\", \".next\", \".nuxt\", \"coverage\", \".tox\", \".eggs\",\n    \"site-packages\", \".terraform\", \"vendor.bundle\",\n}\nEXCLUDED_SUFFIXES = {\n    \".min.js\", \".min.css\", \".map\", \".lock\", \".sum\", \".pyc\", \".pyo\",\n    \".so\", \".o\", \".a\", \".dll\", \".exe\", \".bin\", \".woff\", \".woff2\", \".ttf\",\n    \".eot\", \".otf\", \".png\", \".jpg\", \".jpeg\", \".gif\", \".svg\", \".ico\", \".webp\",\n    \".pdf\", \".zip\", \".gz\", \".tgz\", \".tar\", \".bz2\", \".7z\", \".mp4\", \".mp3\",\n    \".sqlite\", \".db\", \".dump\", \".parquet\", \".feather\", \".h5\", \".pt\",\n    \".pth\", \".onnx\", \".gguf\", \".safetensors\",\n}\nADR_SUFFIXES = (\".md\", \".mdx\")\nADR_DIR_HINTS = (\"adr\", \"decisions\", \"decision-records\")\n\n# Human-acceptance markers for the v1 human-written ADR heuristic (§16.1)\nHUMAN_ADR_MARKERS = (\n    re.compile(r\"status:\\s*accepted.*human\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"\\b(approved[- ]by|accepted[- ]by|reviewed[- ]by|decided[- ]by)\\s*[:\\-]?\\s*\\S+\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"\\bhuman\\s*(approved|accepted|authored|decision)\\b\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"\\bjordan\\b\", re.IGNORECASE),  # product-owner name per agents.md convention\n)\n\n\ndef _token() -> str:\n    return os.environ.get(\"ACMS_GITHUB_ECONOMICS_TOKEN\", \"\")\n\n\ndef _gh(method: str, path: str, timeout: int = 30) -> tuple[int, dict | list | None, str]:\n    req = urllib.request.Request(GITHUB_API + path, method=method)\n    tok = _token()\n    if tok:\n        req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {tok}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Accept\", \"application/vnd.github+json\")\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n            raw = r.read().decode()\n            return r.status, (json.loads(raw) if raw else {}), \"\"\n    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n        try:\n            detail = json.loads(e.read().decode())\n        except Exception:\n            detail = {}\n        return e.code, detail, str(detail.get(\"message\", \"\"))[:200]\n    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n        return 0, None, str(exc)[:200]\n\n\ndef is_code_file(path: str) -> bool:\n    low = path.lower()\n    if low.endswith(tuple(EXCLUDED_SUFFIXES)):\n        return False\n    parts = low.split(\"/\")\n    if any(p in EXCLUDED_DIRS for p in parts[:-1]):\n        return False\n    # count every text-like file; the tree API already excludes nothing, so\n    # this filter decides LOC-worthiness per §16 (exclude vendor/generated)\n    return True\n\n\ndef is_adr_file(path: str) -> bool:\n    low = path.lower()\n    if not low.endswith(ADR_SUFFIXES):\n        return False\n    parts = low.split(\"/\")\n    return any(h in p for p in parts for h in ADR_DIR_HINTS) or parts[-1].startswith((\"adr\", \"adr-\"))\n\n\ndef count_loc(content: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Language-aware-enough v1 LOC: non-empty, non-comment-only lines.\"\"\"\n    n = 0\n....\n74:def _token() -> str:\n78:def _gh(method: str, path: str, timeout: int = 30) -> tuple[int, dict | list | None, str]:\n98:def is_code_file(path: str) -> bool:\n110:def is_adr_file(path: str) -> bool:\n118:def count_loc(content: str) -> int:\n176:    def new_id() -> str:\n182:    def slug(self) -> str:\n224:    def new_id() -> str:\n233:def _fetch_default_branch(owner: str, repo: str) -> tuple[str | None, str]:\n240:def _fetch_tree(owner: str, repo: str, branch: str) -> tuple[list[dict] | None, str]:\n247:def _fetch_file(owner: str, repo: str, path: str, ref: str) -> str | None:\n265:def measure_repository(owner: str, repo: str) -> dict:\n309:def compute_ratios(loc: int | None, total_adr: int | None, human_adr: int | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:\n319:async def get_or_create_repository(db: AsyncSession, owner: str, repo: str,\n335:async def refresh_repository_metrics(db: AsyncSession, repository_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n375:async def product_slop_summary(db: AsyncSession, product_id: str) -> dict:\n410:async def product_slop_history(db: AsyncSession, product_id: str) -> list[dict]:", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

Returns a snapshot dict (never raises\n    for API failures — carries error + partial data).\"\"\"\n    snap: dict = {\"loc\": None, \"total_adr_count\": None, \"human_written_adr_count\": None,\n                  \"human_answer_count\": 0, \"commit_sha\": None, \"error\": None}\n    branch, err = _fetch_default_branch(owner, repo)\n    if branch is None:\n        snap[\"error\"] = f\"default branch: {err}\"[:255]\n        return snap\n    tree, err = _fetch_tree(owner, repo, branch)\n    if tree is None:\n        snap[\"error\"] = f\"tree: {err}\"[:255]\n        return snap\n    snap[\"commit_sha\"] = (tree and tree[0].get(\"sha\")) or None  # proxy; refined below\n    # Real HEAD sha:\n    st, head, _ = _gh(\"GET\", f\"/repos/{owner}/{repo}/commits/{branch}\")\n    if st == 200 and isinstance(head, dict):\n        snap[\"commit_sha\"] = head.get(\"sha\")\n    code_paths = [t[\"path\"] for t in tree\n                  if is_code_file(t[\"path\"]) and not is_adr_file(t[\"path\"])]\n    adr_paths = [t[\"path\"] for t in tree if is_adr_file(t[\"path\"])]\n    if len(code_paths) > 400:\n        snap[\"error\"] = (f\"repository too large for v1 collector ({len(code_paths)} files > cap 400)\")[:255]\n        return snap\n    loc = 0\n    for p in code_paths:\n        content = _fetch_file(owner, repo, p, branch)\n        if content is not None:\n            loc += count_loc(content)\n    total_adr = 0\n    human_adr = 0\n    for p in adr_paths:\n        content = _fetch_file(owner, repo, p, branch)\n        if content is None:\n            continue\n        total_adr += 1\n        if any(m.search(content) for m in HUMAN_ADR_MARKERS):\n            human_adr += 1\n    snap.update({\"loc\": loc, \"total_adr_count\": total_adr,\n                 \"human_written_adr_count\": human_adr,\n                 \"human_answer_count\": human_adr})  # v1: answers == human ADRs (§16.1 stored separately)\n    return snap\n\n\ndef compute_ratios(loc: int | None, total_adr: int | None, human_adr: int | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:\n    \"\"\"True = LOC/human ADRs; Precision = LOC/total ADRs. None when divisor 0/None.\"\"\"\n    true_r = precision_r = None\n    if loc is not None and human_adr:\n        true_r = round(loc / human_adr, 2)\n    if loc is not None and total_adr:\n        precision_r = round(loc / total_adr, 2)\n    return true_r, precision_r\n\n\nasync def get_or_create_repository(db: AsyncSession, owner: str, repo: str,\n                                   canonical_url: str | None = None) -> \"RepositoryRecord\":\n    row = (await db.scalars(\n        select(RepositoryRecord).where(RepositoryRecord.owner == owner,\n                                       RepositoryRecord.repo == repo).limit(1)\n    )).first()\n    if row:\n        return row\n    rec = RepositoryRecord(\n        repository_id=RepositoryRecord.new_id(), owner=owner, repo=repo,\n        canonical_url=canonical_url or f\"https://github.com/{owner}/{repo}\")\n    db.add(rec)\n    await db.flush()\n    return rec\n\n\nasync def refresh_repository_metrics(db: AsyncSession, repository_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Measure linked repos (one, or all when repository_id None) + store snapshots.\"\"\"\n    stmt = select(RepositoryRecord)\n    if repository_id:\n        stmt = stmt.where(RepositoryRecord.repository_id == repository_id)\n    repos = (await db.scalars(stmt)).all()\n    out = []\n    for r in repos:\n        measured = measure_repository(r.owner, r.repo)\n        true_r, precision_r = compute_ratios(\n            measured.get(\"loc\"), measured.get(\"total_adr_count\"),\n            measured.get(\"human_written_adr_count\"))\n        snap = RepositoryMetricSnapshot(\n            snapshot_id=RepositoryMetricSnapshot.new_id(),\n            repository_id=r.repository_id,\n            captured_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n            commit_sha=measured.get(\"commit_sha\"),\n            loc=measured.get(\"loc\"),\n            total_adr_count=measured.get(\"total_adr_count\"),\n            human_written_adr_count=measured.get(\"human_written_adr_count\"),\n            human_answer_count=measured.get(\"human_answer_count\") or 0,\n            true_slop_ratio=true_r,\n            precision_slop_ratio=precision_r,\n            error=measured.get(\"error\"),\n        )\n        db.add(snap)\n        r.last_commit_sha = measured.get(\"commit_sha\")\n        r.last_measured_at = snap.captured_at\n        r.last_loc = measured.get(\"loc\")\n        r.last_total_adr_count = measured.get(\"total_adr_count\")\n        r.last_human_adr_count = measured.get(\"human_written_adr_count\")\n        out.append({\"repository_id\": r.repository_id, \"slug\": r.slug,\n                    \"loc\": measured.get(\"loc\"), \"total_adr\": measured.get(\"total_adr_count\"),\n                    \"human_adr\": measured.get(\"human_written_adr_count\"),\n                    \"true_slop_ratio\": true_r, \"precision_slop_ratio\": precision_r,\n                    \"error\": measured.get(\"error\"), \"snapshot_id\": snap.snapshot_id})\n    await db.commit()\n    return out\n\n\nasync def product_slop_summary(db: AsyncSession, product_id: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Aggregate unique-source Slop summary for one Product (§16 no double-count).\"\"\"\n    from .a2a_models import ProductRecord\n\n    product = await db.get(ProductRecord, product_id)\n    if product is None:\n        return {}\n    rows = (await db.scalars(\n        select(ProductRepositoryLink)\n        .where(ProductRepositoryLink.product_id == product_id))).all()\n    repo_ids = [r.repository_id for r in rows]\n    # unique-source rule: dedupe repository_ids (link table already unique, but\n    # a repo could be linked via multiple projects under the product — §16)\n    latest = {}\n    if repo_ids:\n        snaps = (await db.scalars(\n            select(RepositoryMetricSnapshot)\n            .where(RepositoryMetricSnapshot.repository_id.in_(set(repo_ids)))\n            .order_by(RepositoryMetricSnapshot.captured_at.desc()))).all()\n        for s in snaps:\n            latest.setdefault(s.repository_id, s)  # first per repo = newest\n    total_loc = sum(s.loc or 0 for s in latest.values())\n    total_adr = sum(s.total_adr_count or 0 for s in latest.values())\n    human_adr = sum(s.human_written_adr_count or 0 for s in latest.values())\n    true_r, precision_r = compute_ratios(total_loc, total_adr, human_adr)\n    return {\n        \"product_id\": product_id, \"product_name\": product.name,\n        \"repository_count\": len(latest),\n        \"loc\": total_loc, \"total_adr_count\": total_adr,\n        \"human_written_adr_count\": human_adr,\n        \"true_slop_ratio\": true_r, \"precision_slop_ratio\": precision_r,\n        \"last_measured_at\": max((s.captured_at for s in latest.values()), default=None),\n    }\n\n\nasync def product_slop_history(db: AsyncSession, product_id: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Timeseries for charts (§16): union of member-repo snapshots.\"\"\"\n    rows = (await db.scalars(\n        select(ProductRepositoryLink)\n        .where(ProductRepositoryLink.product_id == product_id))).all()\n    repo_ids = list({r.repository_id for r in rows})\n    if not repo_ids:\n        return []\n s.total_adr_count,\n        \"human_written_adr_count\": s.human_written_adr_count,\n        \"true_slop_ratio\": s.true_slop_ratio,\n        \"precision_slop_ratio\": s.precision_slop_ratio,\n {"output": "\"\"\"Run-event pump (ADR-0014; STEA-004 plan §15).\n\nFor a dispatched run, ACMS subscribes to the worker's Hermes api-server SSE\nstream (`GET /v1/runs/{run_id}/events`) in the background, sanitizes events,\nand persists them to `execution_events` (one row per seq, task-scoped). The\nbrowser never talks to worker VMs; it consumes ACMS-side\n`/api/v1/execution/{task_id}/stream` which replays the persisted rows.\n\nPump lifecycle:\n- scheduled once per (task_id) — idempotent; stop on terminal run status\n- bounded lifetime (RUN_WATCH_MAX_SECONDS class) so dead runs can't pin tasks\n- fail-open: pump errors never break the run or the completion callback\n- the run watcher (completion) stays the terminal-state authority; this pump\n  only provides live visibility.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport urllib.request\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import select\n\nfrom .db import SessionLocal\nfrom .a2a_models import ExecutionEventRecord\nfrom .work_models import ExecutionTaskRecord\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"acms.a2a.run_pump\")\n\n_TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES = {\"completed\", \"failed\", \"cancelled\"}\n_MAX_TASKS_WATCHED = 32\n\n\nclass RunEventPumpRegistry:\n    \"\"\"In-process registry: one pump task per task_id (idempotent scheduling).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self._tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}\n\n    def schedule(self, task_id: str) -> None:\n        if task_id in self._tasks and not self._tasks[task_id].done():\n            return  # already pumping\n        if len([t for t in self._tasks.values() if not t.done()]) >= _MAX_TASKS_WATCHED:\n            logger.warning(\"run-event pump registry saturated; skipping task %s\", task_id)\n            return\n        self._tasks[task_id] = asyncio.create_task(\n            _pump_events(task_id), name=f\"acms-run-pump-{task_id[:8]}\")\n\n    def stop_all(self) -> None:\n        for t in self._tasks.values():\n            t.cancel()\n        self._tasks.clear()\n\n\n_pumps = RunEventPumpRegistry()\n\n\ndef schedule_run_event_pump(task_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Fail-open scheduling (call after commit; errors never break dispatch).\n\n    Disabled when ``ACMS_RUN_EVENT_PUMP_ENABLED=false`` (unit tests use it:\n    a pump polling the shared SQLite DB in a different event loop causes\n    'database is locked' cascades).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        from .settings import get_settings\n\n        if not getattr(get_settings(), \"run_event_pump_enabled\", True):\n            return\n        _pumps.schedule(task_id)\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open by design\n        logger.exception(\"run-event pump scheduling failed for %s\", task_id)\n\n\ndef _bridge_target_for_task(task: ExecutionTaskRecord):\n    \"\"\"Resolve the bridge, verifying it is a real Hermes bridge (has _t with\n    base_url) that can serve run-events. Blocks (executor-run); bounded by resp timeout.\"\"\"\n    events: list[dict] = []\n    buf = b\"\"\n    import time\n\n    start = time.monotonic()\n    while time.monotonic() - start < 25:  # bounded read window per poll\n====\n34:class BridgeError(Exception):\n38:def _post_json(url: str, key: str, body: dict | None = None, method: str = \"POST\",\n56:def _get_json(base_url: str, key: str, path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n69:class BridgeTarget:\n76:def load_targets() -> list[BridgeTarget]:\n91:def get_bridge_for_agent(agent_id: str):\n109:class HermesBridge:\n113:    def __init__(self, target: BridgeTarget):\n118:    def fetch_status(self) -> HeartbeatPayload:\n148:    def send_work(self, *, work_key: str, assignment_key: str, instruction: str,\n175:    def steer(self, session_id: str, message: str) -> dict:\n181:    def interrupt(self, run_id: str) -> dict:\n188:    def set_session_title(self, session_id: str, title: str) -> dict:\n192:    def request_handoff(self, session_id: str, work_key: str) -> dict:\n205:    def _get(self, path: str) -> dict:", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "while time.monotonic() - start < 25:  # bounded read window per poll\n        chunk = resp.read1(8192)\n        if not chunk:\n            break\n        buf += chunk\n        lines = buf.split(b\"\\n\\n\")\n        buf = lines.pop()  # tail\n        for block in lines:\n            for line in block.split(b\"\\n\"):\n                if line.startswith(b\"data: \"):\n                    try:\n                        events.append(json.loads(line[6:].decode()))\n                    except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError):\n                        continue\n        if any(e.get(\"event\", \"\").startswith(\"run.\") for e in events):\n            break\n    return events or None\n\n\ndef _sanitize(ev: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Same allow-list as the ingestion API (ADR-0014 §14: no secrets/reasoning).\"\"\"\n    out = {}\n    for key in (\"event\", \"run_id\", \"tool\", \"duration\", \"error\"):\n        if ev.get(key) is not None:\n            out[key] = ev[key]\n    for key, cap in ((\"preview\", 500), (\"delta\", 500), (\"output\", 2000)):\n        if ev.get(key) is not None:\n            out[key] = str(ev[key])[:cap]\n    if isinstance(ev.get(\"usage\"), dict):\n        out[\"usage\"] = {k: ev[\"usage\"].get(k) for k in (\"input_tokens\", \"output_tokens\", \"total_tokens\")\n                        if ev[\"usage\"].get(k) is not None}\n    return out\n\n\nasync def _pump_events(task_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Poll the run status; while the run is live, pull the SSE stream window\n    and persist any new events. Poll-based (not persistent-stream) so ACMS\n    restarts resume cleanly and the pump never holds a socket for hours.\"\"\"\n    from .settings import get_settings\n\n    poll_s = 5\n    unresolvable_rounds = 0\n    import time as _time\n\n    started = _time.monotonic()\n    max_seconds = 7200  # hard lifetime cap (matches run-watch bound)\n    try:\n        from .settings import get_settings as _gs\n        max_seconds = _gs().callback_watch_max_seconds\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n    while True:\n        if _time.monotonic() - started > max_seconds:\n            logger.info(\"run pump lifetime cap reached for task %s\", task_id)\n            return\n        async with SessionLocal() as db:\n            task = await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, task_id)\n            if task is None or task.status != \"RUNNING\" or not task.external_task_id:\n                return\n            bridge = _bridge_target_for_task(task)\n            run_id = task.external_task_id\n        if bridge is None:\n            # unresolvable/unsupported bridge: exit immediately (fail-open —\n            # the completion watcher + reconcile remain the terminal authorities)\n            return\n        # blocking HTTP in executor to keep the loop healthy\n        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()\n\n        def _fetch():\n            if bridge is None or bridge._t is None:\n                return None\n            url = f\"{bridge._t.base_url}/v1/runs/{run_id}/events\"\n            req = urllib.request.Request(url)\n            req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {bridge._t.api_key}\")\n            try:\n                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:\n                    return _sse_events(resp)\n            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n                logger.debug(\"run pump fetch %s: %s\", run_id[:16], e)\n                return None\n\n        events = await loop.run_in_executor(None, _fetch)\n        if events:\n            async with SessionLocal() as db:\n                max_seq = (await db.execute(\n                    select(ExecutionEventRecord.seq)\n                    .where(ExecutionEventRecord.task_id == task_id)\n                    .order_by(ExecutionEventRecord.seq.desc()).limit(1))).scalar()\n                seq = max_seq or 0\n                new = 0\n                from datetime import datetime, timezone\n\n                now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n                for ev in events:\n                    if not isinstance(ev, dict):\n                        continue\n                    seq += 1\n                    etype = str(ev.get(\"event\") or \"unknown\")[:64]\n                    db.add(ExecutionEventRecord(\n                        task_id=task_id, seq=seq, event_type=etype,\n                        occurred_at=now,\n                        payload_json=json.dumps(_sanitize(ev))[:8000]))\n                    new += 1\n                if new:\n                    await db.commit()\n                terminal = any(\n                    isinstance(e, dict) and e.get(\"event\") in\n                    (\"run.completed\", \"run.failed\", \"run.cancelled\")\n                    for e in events if isinstance(e, dict))\n                if terminal:\n                    return\n        else:\n            # no events visible: stop when the run is already terminal (cheap\n            # status check) — the SSE endpoint 404s after run TTL anyway.\n            async with SessionLocal() as db:\n                t2 = await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, task_id)\n                if t2 is None or t2.status != \"RUNNING\":\n                    return\n        await asyncio.sleep(poll_s)=======\n151:        chat (existing session); includes ACMS keys in metadata per REQ-052.\n156:        Session-bound chat cannot change the model (Hermes session override\n162:            return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n176:        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

Every method here corresponds to a\n111|    row in docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md.\"\"\"\n112|\n113|    def __init__(self, target: BridgeTarget):\n114|        self._t = target\n115|\n116|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- status\n117|\n118|    def fetch_status(self) -> HeartbeatPayload:\n119|        \"\"\"Full status snapshot via the tested mapping (reconciliation path).\n120|\n121|        Raises BridgeError when unreachable — the scheduler maps that to\n122|        UNREACHABLE per ADR-0010.\"\"\"\n123|        sessions = self._get(\"/api/sessions\")\n124|        rows = sessions.get(\"data\") or sessions.get(\"sessions\") or []\n125|        if not rows:\n126|            raise BridgeError(\"no sessions reported\")\n127|        # Most recently active session = the agent's current work context.\n128|        rows.sort(key=lambda s: s.get(\"last_active\") or 0, reverse=True)\n129|        cur = rows[0]\n130|        sess = self._get(f\"/api/sessions/{cur['id']}\").get(\"session\", {})\n131|        return HeartbeatPayload(\n132|            schema_version=\"acms-heartbeat-v1\",\n133|            identity=HeartbeatIdentity(\n134|                acms_agent_id=self._t.agent_id,\n135|                bridge_id=\"hermes-api-server\",\n136|                harness=\"hermes\",\n137|            ),\n138|            session=HeartbeatSession(\n139|                session_id=str(sess.get(\"id\") or cur.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n140|                title=sess.get(\"title\"),\n141|                agent_running=False,  # sessions API has no live running flag; reconciliation is contact-only\n142|            ),\n143|            platforms={\"connected\": [\"api_server\"]},\n144|        )\n145|\n146|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- controls\n147|\n148|    def send_work(self, *, work_key: str, assignment_key: str, instruction: str,\n149|                  session_id: str | None = None, model: str | None = None) -> dict:\n150|        \"\"\"Dispatch work. Uses run submission (new session) or session-bound\n151|        chat (existing session); includes ACMS keys in metadata per REQ-052.\n152|\n153|        ``model`` (ADR-0015): the ACMS-resolved effective model. For new runs\n154|        it rides the /v1/runs ``model`` field — the worker honors it when it\n155|        has a matching model_routes entry (worker config owns that mapping).\n156|        Session-bound chat cannot change the model (Hermes session override\n157|        rules); the request is still accepted — the policy travels in the\n158|        envelope for operator visibility.\"\"\"\n159|        meta = {\"acms_work_key\": work_key, \"acms_assignment_key\": assignment_key,\n160|                \"source\": \"acms\"}\n161|        if session_id:\n162|            return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n163|                              self._t.api_key,\n164|                              {\"message\": instruction},\n165|                              headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n166|        body: dict = {\n167|            \"input\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": instruction}],\n168|            \"metadata\": meta,\n169|            \"title\": f\"[{work_key}] ACMS dispatched work\",\n170|        }\n171|        if model:\n172|            body[\"model\"] = model\n173|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs\", self._t.api_key, body)\n174|\n175|    def steer(self, session_id: str, message: str) -> dict:\n176|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n177|                          self._t.api_key,\n178|                          {\"message\": message},\n179|                          headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n180|\n181|    def interrupt(self, run_id: str) -> dict:\n182|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs/{run_id}/stop\", self._t.api_key)\n183|\n184|    # `cancel` uses the same tested terminal endpoint as interrupt (A2A-task\n185|    # level); the Hermes session survives — new execution task to continue.\n186|    cancel = interrupt\n187|\n188|    def set_session_title(self, session_id: str, title: str) -> dict:\n189|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}\",\n190|                          self._t.api_key, {\"title\": title}, method=\"PATCH\")\n191|\n192|    def request_handoff(self, session_id: str, work_key: str) -> dict:\n193|        \"\"\"Plan §10: structured Markdown handoff request (via send_work).\"\"\"\n194|        instruction = (\n195|            f\"Produce a structured Markdown handoff for [{work_key}]: what you \"\n196|            \"believe you are doing, current goal, actions completed, \"\n197|            \"artifacts/files changed, blockers, and next intended action. \"\n198|            \"Use 'Observed:' vs 'Interpretation:' conventions.\"\n199|        )\n200|        return self.send_work(work_key=work_key, assignment_key=\"\",\n201|                              instruction=instruction, session_id=session_id)\n202|\n203|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers\n204|\n205|    def _get(self, path: str) -> dict:\n206|        return _get_json(self._t.base_url, self._t.api_key, path)\n207|", "total_lines": 206, "file_size": 8748, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"success": true, "name": "acms-project-operations", "file": "references/hermes-019-worker-api-surface-2026-10-01.md", "content": "# Hermes 0.19.0 worker api-server surface — verified live 2026-10-01\n\nProbed against acms-worker-001 (VM124 @ 10.0.20.203:8402, hermes-agent 0.19.0\nin `/opt/hermes-venv`, gateway = `hermes-bridge.service` running\n`hermes gateway run --profile acms-worker-001 --replace`). Everything below\nwas verified with real HTTP calls from the ACMS container; the worker needed\nZERO changes for the A2A production path.\n\n## Machine-readable capability list\n`GET /v1/capabilities` → auth, `runtime.mode=server_agent`, feature flags\n(run_events_sse, run_stop, tool_progress_events, session_chat_streaming,\nsession_fork, skills_api, …), and an `endpoints` map with exact paths/methods.\nProbe this FIRST instead of guessing routes.\n\n## Run lifecycle (the A2A ACK)\n- `POST /v1/runs` body `{input:[{role,content}], metadata, title, model?}` →\n  **202 `{run_id, status:\"started\"}`** — the run_id IS the worker ACK\n  (ADR-0013 contract).\n- `GET /v1/runs/{run_id}` → `{status: running|completed|failed|cancelled,\n  output, usage{input_tokens,output_tokens,total_tokens}, last_event,\n  session_id, created_at, updated_at}`. **Run statuses are TTL'd** — old run\n  ids 404 after a while; don't treat a 404 as \"never ran\".\n- `POST /v1/runs/{run_id}/stop` = interrupt/cancel (one endpoint).\n- **Model-field caveat:** the request `model` only takes effect through\n  `platforms.api_server.extra.model_routes` (alias → `{model, provider?,\n  api_key?, base_url?}`) in the worker profile config. An UNMATCHED alias is\n  silently ignored → the agent runs the profile config default. Verified live:\n  `model=qwen3.8-flash-next` without a model_routes entry ran `fast`\n  (= qwen3.6). Worker-side fix = add model_routes entries; ACMS-side =\n  still send the effective model in the request body\n  (`bridge.send_work(model=...)`) so the intent is auditable.\n\n## Run events SSE — `GET /v1/runs/{run_id}/events`\ntext/event-stream, `data: {json}` lines, ~30s `: keepalive` comments, stream\ncloses after the terminal event. Verified payload shapes:\n- `run.completed` `{output, usage{input_tokens,output_tokens,total_tokens}}`\n- `run.failed` `{error (redacted)}`, `run.cancelled`\n- `tool.started` `{tool, preview}`, `tool.completed` `{tool, duration, error}`\n- `message.delta` `{delta}`, `reasoning.available` `{text}`\nSubscribe right after POST (handler waits up to 1s for stream registration).\n`reasoning.available` carries hidden model reasoning — ACMS drops it\n(STEA-004 plan §14: never expose non-user-visible reasoning).\n\n## Session chat stream — `POST /api/sessions/{id}/chat/stream`\nSSE events: run.started, message.started, assistant.delta, tool.progress,\ntool.started/completed/failed, assistant.completed, run.completed, error,\ndone. Response header `X-Hermes-Session-Id` echoes the session id.\n\n## Health / status (heartbeat sources)\n- `GET /health/detailed` → `{status, readiness.checks{state_db, config,\n  model, disk{used_percent,free_bytes}, gateway{state,connected_platforms},\n  background_queues{active_api_runs,process_completions,\n  active_delegations}}, active_agents, gateway_busy, gateway_drainable,\n  platforms.api_server{state}, version, updated_at}`.\n  `active_agents > 0 or gateway_busy` ⇒ worker is executing.\n- `GET /api/sessions` rows carry: id, model, title, started_at/ended_at,\n  message_count, tool_call_count, input_tokens, output_tokens,\n  cache_read_tokens, cache_write_tokens, reasoning_tokens,\n  estimated_cost_usd, actual_cost_usd, api_call_count, last_active, preview —\n  one call yields session identity + token usage + cost for the heartbeat\n  payload (what `telemetry_scheduler._fetch_worker_status` consumes).\n- `GET /api/sessions/{id}/messages` → role, content, reasoning, token_count,\n  timestamp (transcript excerpts for Context Markdown).\n\n## Route truth for model-policy chains (ACMS side)\nServer Manager (VM114 :8300) `GET /api/v1/model-routes` (svc-token auth —\n`svc-server-manager` from service_tokens; split `token:scopes` on the FIRST\ncolon) returns every logical route with `{engine, backend_url, health,\nroutable, is_alias, alias_target, context_limit}`. Healthy locals =\n`routable && !is_alias && health==\"healthy\" && engine != openrouter`.\nThis is what `acms/model_policy.py::fetch_healthy_local_models()` consumes —\nLLM Manager owns route truth; ACMS never maintains its own health copy.", "file_type": ".md"}

break at the last sentence boundary inside the cap when possible\n    for sep in (\". \", \"! \", \"? \"):\n        idx = cut.rfind(sep)\n        if idx > 40:\n            return cut[: idx + 1]\n    return cut[:117] + \"...\"\n\n\nasync def create_inbox_item(\n    db: AsyncSession, *, title: str, summary: str,\n    item_class: str = \"ACTION_REQUIRED\", severity: str = \"medium\",\n    agent_id: str | None = None, work_item_id: str | None = None,\n    execution_session_id: str | None = None, project_id: str | None = None,\n    product_id: str | None = None, jira_issue_key: str | None = None,\n    correlation_id: str | None = None, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> HumanInboxItemRecord:\n    \"\"\"Create one inbox row. correlation_id dedupes an OPEN item of the same\n    trigger (related events aggregate into one item per plan §23).\"\"\"\n    if item_class not in ITEM_CLASSES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown inbox class {item_class!r}\")\n    if severity not in SEVERITIES:\n        severity = \"medium\"\n    if correlation_id:\n        existing = (await db.scalars(\n            select(HumanInboxItemRecord).where(\n                HumanInboxItemRecord.correlation_id == correlation_id,\n                HumanInboxItemRecord.archived_at.is_(None),\n            ).order_by(HumanInboxItemRecord.created_at.desc()).limit(1)\n        )).first()\n        if existing is not None:\n            # aggregate: refresh the summary/timestamp, keep one row\n            existing.summary = _caveman_summary(summary) or existing.summary\n            existing.updated_at = HumanInboxItemRecord.now()\n            if metadata:\n                try:\n                    merged = json.loads(existing.metadata_json or \"{}\")\n                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                    merged = {}\n                merged.update(metadata)\n                existing.metadata_json = json.dumps(merged)\n            await db.commit()\n            await db.refresh(existing)\n            return existing\n    rec = HumanInboxItemRecord(\n        item_id=HumanInboxItemRecord.new_id(),\n        item_class=item_class,\n        severity=severity,\n        title=title[:255],\n        summary=_caveman_summary(summary),\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        work_item_id=work_item_id,\n        execution_session_id=execution_session_id,\n        project_id=project_id,\n        product_id=product_id,\n        jira_issue_key=jira_issue_key,\n        correlation_id=correlation_id,\n        metadata_json=json.dumps(metadata) if metadata else None,\n        created_at=HumanInboxItemRecord.now(),\n        updated_at=HumanInboxItemRecord.now(),\n    )\n    db.add(rec)\n    await db.commit()\n    await db.refresh(rec)\n    return rec\n\n\nasync def demote_for_work_item(\n    db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str, *, to_class: str = \"STALE\",\n    reason: str = \"linked execution resolved\",\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Auto-demotion (plan §23): ACTION_REQUIRED items whose execution finished\n    or found another path become FYI/STALE. Returns demoted count.\"\"\"\n    if to_class not in ITEM_CLASSES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown inbox class {to_class!r}\")\n    rows = (await db.scalars(\n        select(HumanInboxItemRecord).where(\n            HumanInboxItemRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id,\n            HumanInboxItemRecord.item_class == \"ACTION_REQUIRED\",\n            HumanInboxItemRecord.archived_at.is_(None),\n        )\n    )).all()\n    now = HumanInboxItemRecord.now()\n    for r in rows:\n        r.item_class = to_class\n        r.updated_at = now\n        r.metadata_json = json.dumps({\"demoted_reason\": reason, **_safe_json(r.metadata_json)})\n    if rows:\n        await db.commit()\n    return len(rows)\n\n\ndef _safe_json(raw: str | None) -> dict:\n    try:\n        data = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}\n        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return {}\n\n\nasync def list_inbox(\n    db: AsyncSession, *, unread_only: bool = False, item_class: str | None = None,\n    severity: str | None = None, agent_id: str | None = None,\n    project_id: str | None = None, include_archived: bool
