{"output": "\"\"\"Attention/audit service — Phase 10 slice 6 foundation (REV2 plan §15).\n\nSemantic Attention candidates derived from the durable event log (never token\nnoise). SSE transport ships separately; this slice ships the derived Attention\nfeed + API so the UI and any SSE layer have one authority to subscribe to.\n\nAttention rules (REV2 §15 priority list, mapped to durable events):\n  UNREACHABLE / recovery exhausted → AgentEvent UNREACHABLE / RECONCILE_* failures\n  failed/rejected execution        → execution task FAILED + A2A rejects\n  handoff requesting human input   → handoff event with human_input flag\n  mandatory approval               → APPROVAL_REQUESTED events\n  Hermes state-backup failure      → STATE_SYNC_FAILED\n  hard budget reached              → BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED (HARD)\n  budget override required         → BUDGET_OVERRIDE (already an audit fact)\n  session rotation required        → SESSION_ROTATION_REQUIRED\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\nfrom sqlalchemy import or_, select\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom .db import get_session\nfrom .security import require_admin_token\nfrom .telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api/v1/attention\", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_token)])\n\n# Attention-eligible event types (semantic state changes only)\nATTENTION_EVENT_TYPES = (\n    \"UNREACHABLE\",\n    \"RECONCILE_FAILED\",\n    \"RUNTIME_RECOVERY_EXHAUSTED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n    \"HANDOFF_HUMAN_INPUT\",\n    \"APPROVAL_REQUESTED\",\n    \"STATE_SYNC_FAILED\",\n    \"BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED\",\n    \"BUDGET_OVERRIDE\",\n    \"SESSION_ROTATION_REQUIRED\",\n    \"RUNTIME_STOPPED_WITH_ACTIVE_WORK\",\n    \"AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT\",\n    \"REASSIGNMENT_LIMIT_REACHED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_COMPLETED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_CANCELLED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED\",\n    \"EXECUTION_RECONCILED\",\n)\n\n# Severity mapping (REV2 §15 priority order: highest first)\nSEVERITY = {\n    \"UNREACHABLE\": \"critical\",\n    \"RUNTIME_RECOVERY_EXHAUSTED\": \"critical\",\n    \"REASSIGNMENT_LIMIT_REACHED\": \"high\",\n    \"AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT\": \"info\",\n    \"EXECUTION_REJECTED\": \"high\",\n    \"EXECUTION_FAILED\": \"high\",\n    \"APPROVAL_REQUESTED\": \"high\",\n    \"HANDOFF_HUMAN_INPUT\": \"high\",\n    \"STATE_SYNC_FAILED\": \"high\",\n    \"BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED\": \"high\",\n    \"BUDGET_OVERRIDE\": \"medium\",\n    \"SESSION_ROTATION_REQUIRED\": \"medium\",\n    \"RECONCILE_FAILED\": \"medium\",\n    \"RUNTIME_STOPPED_WITH_ACTIVE_WORK\": \"medium\",\n    \"EXECUTION_CANCELLED\": \"medium\",\n    \"EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED\": \"high\",\n    \"EXECUTION_RECONCILED\": \"info\",\n    # EXECUTION_COMPLETED intentionally absent: success → no Attention item\n    # (it stays in the event log + SSE; ADR-0012 Attention policy).\n}\n\nDEFAULT_LOOKBACK_HOURS = 72\n\n\nclass AttentionItem(BaseModel):\n    event_id: str\n    sequence: int\n    timestamp: str | None\n    event_type: str\n    severity: str\n    agent_id: str | None\n    summary: str\n    acknowledged: bool = False\n\n\ndef _derive(r: AgentEventRecord, severity: str | None):\n    \"\"\"Map one durable event row to an Attention dict (shared API+UI).\"\"\"\n    sev = SEVERITY.get(r.event_type, \"medium\")\n    if r.event_type == \"BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED\" and \"HARD_EXCEEDED\" in (r.summary or \"\"):\n        sev = \"critical\"\n    if r.event_type == \"EXECUTION_COMPLETED\":\n        return None  # success never surfaces as Attention (ADR-0012 policy)\n    if severity and sev != severity:\n        return None\n    return {\n        \"event_id\": r.event_id,\n        \"sequence\": r.sequence,\n        \"timestamp\": r.timestamp.isoformat() if r.timestamp else None,\n        \"timestamp_display\": r.timestamp.strftime(\"%m-%d %H:%M\") if r.timestamp else \"—\",\n        \"event_type\": r.event_type,\n        \"severity\": sev,\n        \"agent_id\": r.agent_id,\n        \"work_key\": r.work_key,\n        \"assignment_key\": r.assignment_key,\n        \"summary\": r.summary,\n    }\n\n\nasync def attention_items(db: AsyncSession, *, hours: int = DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_HOURS,\n                          severity: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Derived Attention feed as plain dicts — one derivation shared by the\n    JSON API, the UI page, and any future consumer (single source of truth).\"\"\"\n    since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)\n    stmt = (\n        select(AgentEventRecord)\n        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type.in_(ATTENTION_EVENT_TYPES))\n        .where(AgentEventRecord.timestamp >= since)\n        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.sequence.desc())\n        .limit(200)\n    )\n    rows = (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()\n    items = []\n    for r in rows:\n        d = _derive(r, severity)\n        if d is not None:\n            items.append(d)\n    return items\n\n\n@router.get(\"\", response_model=list[AttentionItem])\nasync def attention_feed(\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n    hours: int = Query(default=DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_HOURS, ge=1, le=24 * 30),\n    severity: str | None = Query(default=None),\n    include_acknowledged: bool = False,\n):\n    \"\"\"Derived Attention feed: recent semantic events that need human/Executive\n    eyes. Deterministic derivation from the audit log — no separate state to\n    drift out of sync. BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED items whose metadata says HARD\n    rank high; the summary already carries the state.\"\"\"\n    dicts = await attention_items(db, hours=hours, severity=severity)\n    return [AttentionItem(**{k: d[k] for k in\n                             (\"event_id\", \"sequence\", \"timestamp\", \"event_type\",\n                              \"severity\", \"agent_id\", \"summary\")}) for d in dicts]", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "attention_api.py\nwork_board.py\n\"\"\"Attention UI — operator view over the derived Attention feed (REV2 Phase D).\n\nRead-only view backed by attention_items() (the same derivation the JSON API\nand SSE use). No mutable duplicate Attention state is created here;\nacknowledgement/resolution, if required later, becomes an auditable action\nlayered on the durable event source (plan §6-D1).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom ..attention_api import attention_items\nfrom ..db import get_session\nfrom .session_auth import current_user\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/ui/attention\")\n\n_SEV_ORDER = {\"critical\": 0, \"high\": 1, \"medium\": 2, \"low\": 3}\n\n\n@router.get(\"\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\n@router.get(\"/\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def attention_page(\n    request: Request,\n    severity: str | None = Query(default=None),\n    hours: int = Query(default=72, ge=1, le=24 * 30),\n    user=Depends(current_user),\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n):\n    items = await attention_items(db, hours=hours, severity=severity)\n    items.sort(key=lambda i: (_SEV_ORDER.get(i[\"severity\"], 9),\n                              -(i[\"sequence\"] or 0)))\n    from .routes import templates\n\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request,\n        \"attention.html\",\n        {\"user\": user, \"items\": items, \"severity\": severity, \"hours\": hours},\n    )", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"content": "30|\n31|# Semantic event types eligible for streaming (plan §6-D2 recommended set).\n32|# Everything here is a material state change; heartbeat/token telemetry never\n33|# appears in agent_events, but the allow-list also guards future accidents.\n34|# (2026-09-29) + recovery-guardrail events: AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT,\n35|# REASSIGNMENT_LIMIT_REACHED, REASSIGNMENT_RECORDED (Phase C4/C5).\n36|SSE_EVENT_TYPES = (\n37|    \"UNREACHABLE\",\n38|    \"RUNTIME_RECOVERY_EXHAUSTED\",\n39|    \"RECONCILE_FAILED\",\n40|    \"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\",\n41|    \"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n42|    \"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n43|    \"HANDOFF_HUMAN_INPUT\",\n44|    \"APPROVAL_REQUESTED\",\n45|    \"STATE_SYNC_FAILED\",\n46|    \"BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED\",\n47|    \"BUDGET_OVERRIDE\",\n48|    \"SESSION_ROTATION_REQUIRED\",\n49|    \"RUNTIME_STOPPED_WITH_ACTIVE_WORK\",\n50|    \"AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT\",\n51|    \"REASSIGNMENT_RECORDED\",\n52|    \"REASSIGNMENT_REFUSED\",\n53|    \"REASSIGNMENT_LIMIT_REACHED\",\n54|    # ADR-0012 completion-callback events (2026-09-29).\n55|    \"EXECUTION_COMPLETED\",\n56|    \"EXECUTION_CANCELLED\",\n57|    \"EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED\",\n58|    \"EXECUTION_RECONCILED\",\n59|)\n60|\n61|HEARTBEAT_S = 15\n62|POLL_S = 2.0\n63|\n64|\n65|def _sse_line(event_id: int, event_type: str, payload: dict) -> str:\n66|    data = json.dumps(payload, separators=(\",\", \":\"))\n67|    return f\"id: {event_id}\\nevent: {event_type}\\ndata: {data}\\n\\n\"\n68|\n69|\n70|async def _event_stream(request: Request, last_sequence: int,\n71|                        max_events: int | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:\n72|    from .db import SessionLocal\n73|\n74|    yield \": connected (ACMS semantic event stream)\\n\\n\"\n75|    cursor = last_sequence\n76|    sent = 0\n77|    while True:\n78|        if await request.is_disconnected():\n79|            return\n80|        async with SessionLocal() as db:\n81|            rows = (await db.execute(\n82|                select(AgentEventRecord)\n83|                .where(AgentEventRecord.sequence.isnot(None))\n84|                .where(AgentEventRecord.sequence > cursor)\n85|                .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type.in_(SSE_EVENT_TYPES))\n86|                .order_by(AgentEventRecord.sequence.asc())\n87|                .limit(100)\n88|            )).scalars().all()\n89|            if max_events is not None and not rows:\n90|                return  # bounded consumer drained; close cleanly\n91|            for r in rows:\n92|                cursor = r.sequence\n93|                sent += 1\n94|                yield _sse_line(r.sequence, r.event_type, {\n95|                    \"event_id\": r.event_id,\n96|                    \"sequence\": r.sequence,\n97|                    \"timestamp\": r.timestamp.isoformat() if r.timestamp else None,\n98|                    \"event_type\": r.event_type,\n99|                    \"agent_id\": r.agent_id,\n100|                    \"work_key\": r.work_key,\n101|                    \"assignment_key\": r.assignment_key,\n102|                    \"a2a_task_id\": r.a2a_task_id,\n103|                    \"summary\": r.summary,\n104|                })\n105|", "total_lines": 141, "file_size": 5399, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=105 to continue reading (showing 30-104 of 141 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

All tests below were\nexecuted live on 2026-09-26/27 against that instance.\n\n## Capability matrix — Hermes 0.17.0 (api-server transport)\n\n| ACMS capability | Required semantics | Harness mapping (tested) | Tested version | Result |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| status | full current status | `GET /api/sessions/{id}` (id, title, model, message_count, input/output tokens, api_call_count) + `GET /v1/runs/{run_id}` (status, last_event, usage, output) + `GET /api/sessions` (last_active per session) | 0.17.0 f3d2dfb | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested) |\n| send_work | dispatch controlled work | `POST /v1/runs` (input, metadata) or `POST /api/sessions/{id}/chat` + `X-Hermes-Session-Id` header for session-bound dispatch; returns run_id/session_id immediately (202 semantics) | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested) |\n| steer | inject direction into active run | `POST /api/sessions/{id}/chat` with `X-Hermes-Session-Id` while run is active — message lands in-session (verified: \"STEER: stop at 10\" → agent acknowledged mid-run \"Stopping there… Summary of what I was doing\") | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested) |\n| pause | resumable suspension of same unit | no HTTP endpoint (`POST /v1/runs/{id}/pause` → 404); only cron-job pause exists (`/api/jobs/{id}/pause`) — different domain | 0.17.0 | **UNSUPPORTED** (404 live) — never fake with interrupt |\n| resume | continue same paused unit | n/a (no pause) | 0.17.0 | **UNSUPPORTED** |\n| interrupt | stop current run/turn, preserve session | `POST /v1/runs/{id}/stop` → `agent.interrupt(\"Stop requested via API\")`; status running→stopping→cancelled; **session preserved** (verified: messages intact, follow-up message in same session answered with full context \"the run was interrupted before any output reached me\") | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested) |\n| cancel | terminal A2A task cancellation | same endpoint as interrupt (`/stop`); the A2A execution task terminates (status `cancelled`); the Hermes *session* survives, so continuing the Work Item later = new execution task bound to the same session | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested; terminal at A2A-task level) |\n| request_handoff | request Markdown handoff | no dedicated endpoint; implemented as a dispatched instruction via `send_work` (\"produce a Markdown handoff…\") with the reply captured from run output/messages | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED (via send_work)** — degrade gracefully if run fails |\n| set_session_title | set work-linked title | `PATCH /api/sessions/{id}` `{\"title\": \"[ACMS-WORK-…] …\"}` (verified live; allowed fields exactly `title`, `end_reason`) | 0.17.0 | **SUPPORTED** (live-tested) |\n\n## Other live-verified facts (plan §14 test log)\n\n| # | Test | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Version/commit | Hermes 0.17.0, commit f3d2dfbec670 (2026-06-30) |\n| 2 | Status before execution | `/api/sessions` + `/v1/runs/{id}` give full inventory |\n| 3 | Dispatch ACMS-keyed task | `POST /v1/runs` with metadata accepted (202, run_id immediate) |\n| 4 | Title + session id | `session_id == run_id` for run submissions; title set via PATCH stuck (\"[ACMS-WORK-000042] Bridge control live test\") |\n| 5 | Steering | Works into a running session via session chat (verified mid-run) |\n| 6 | Stop/interrupt + continue | `/stop` → `cancelled`; same-session follow-up works; agent knows it was interrupted |\n| 7 | True Pause/Resume | **NOT AVAILABLE** at run level (404). `/busy` queue/steer/interrupt exists; `/platform pause` is messaging-only. Do not fake. |\n| 8 | `/new` behavior | `POST /api/sessions` creates explicit new sessions (verified `acms-explicit-session-01`) |\n| 9 | Terminal cancel mapping | run-level `/stop` = terminal for the run; session reusable |\n| 10 | Context telemetry sources | run status `usage` (input/output/total per run); session record `input_tokens/output_tokens/cache_*/api_call_count`; **context-window max NOT exposed** — ACMS must get max from LLM Manager model metadata or configured fallback (plan §6: never fabricate) |\n\n## Notes / gaps for architecture review\n\n- No native Pause/Resume at the agent-turn level (plan §5.2 respected:\n  reported as unsupported rather than faked).\n- `agent_running` is not directly exposed by api-server; derive from run\n  status (`running` → true, else false) plus session `last_active` freshness.\n- Heartbeat context fields: prefer run/session token accounting\n  (`input_tokens` of latest turn) with `max_source` recorded; unknown max →\n  `UNKNOWN/INVALID` per REQ-054.\n- The installed stack is 0.17.0 (2026-06-30) — older than upstream docs I\n  consulted; behavior verified against the installed build per plan §14.\n- Upstream research inputs (unverified for this version) that matched the\n  installed tree: `/status` prints Session ID/Title/Model/Tokens/Agent\n  Running; `/busy steer` exists as a CLI-level steering path; gateway\n  `agent.interrupt()` preserves session (code path verified in\n  `run_agent.py` `interrupt()` + live stop test).\n57", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "\"\"\"Human-readable immutable UIDs (STEA-004 plan §6/§7; ADR-0017 identity rules).\n\nTwo UID families, both backed by the durable ``acms_key_counters`` table\n(never COUNT(*), never reused — the counter only increments):\n\n- Work UID:  ``ACMS-WORK-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS`` (§6)\n- Artifact UID: ``ACMS-ARTIFACT-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS`` (§7)\n\nDesign decisions (recorded for reviewers):\n\n- Work UIDs share the SAME ``work_key`` counter as the short REQ-052 key so\n  ``ACMS-WORK-000127`` and ``ACMS-WORK-000127-20261001_082241`` are the same\n  number for the same row — session-alignment machinery (telemetry_service\n  extracts short keys from harness titles) stays compatible, and the UID is\n  a pure extension of the existing identity rather than a second identity.\n- Artifact UIDs use a NEW ``artifact_uid`` counter (artifacts had no\n  human-readable key before this release).\n- The timestamp portion is the row's ``created_at`` rendered in UTC —\n  computed ONCE at allocation; the full UID is immutable afterwards.\n- Internal UUIDs remain the immutable database identity (REQ-001 discipline).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import text\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nWORK_UID_PREFIX = \"ACMS-WORK-\"\nARTIFACT_UID_PREFIX = \"ACMS-ARTIFACT-\"\nCOUNTER_WORK = \"work_key\"  # shared with the REQ-052 short key (same sequence)\nCOUNTER_ARTIFACT = \"artifact_uid\"\n\n\ndef _ts_portion(ts: datetime | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"created_at rendered as YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS in UTC (naive == treated as UTC).\"\"\"\n    if ts is None:\n        ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n    if ts.tzinfo is None:\n        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n    return ts.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\n\n\ndef format_uid(prefix: str, seq: int, ts: datetime | None) -> str:\n    return f\"{prefix}{seq:06d}-{_ts_portion(ts)}\"\n\n\nasync def _next_counter(db: AsyncSession, name: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Atomically increment and return the counter (same mechanism as work_keys).\"\"\"\n    await db.execute(\n        text(\n            \"INSERT INTO acms_key_counters (counter_name, counter_value) \"\n            \"VALUES (:name, 0) ON CONFLICT (counter_name) DO NOTHING\"\n        ),\n        {\"name\": name},\n    )\n    row = await db.execute(\n        text(\n            \"UPDATE acms_key_counters SET counter_value = counter_value + 1 \"\n            \"WHERE counter_name = :name RETURNING counter_value\"\n        ),\n        {\"name\": name},\n    )\n    return int(row.scalar_one())\n\n\nasync def allocate_work_uid(db: AsyncSession, created_at: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, str]:\n    \"\"\"Allocate the next work sequence and return ``(seq, full_uid)``.\"\"\"\n    seq = await _next_counter(db, COUNTER_WORK)\n    return seq, format_uid(WORK_UID_PREFIX, seq, created_at)\n\n\nasync def allocate_artifact_uid(db: AsyncSession, created_at: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, str]:\n    \"\"\"Allocate the next artifact sequence and return ``(seq, full_uid)``.\"\"\"\n    seq = await _next_counter(db, COUNTER_ARTIFACT)\n    return seq, format_uid(ARTIFACT_UID_PREFIX, seq, created_at)\n\n\ndef work_uid_from_key(work_key: str | None, created_at: datetime | None) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Derive the long-form UID from an existing short key (backfill path).", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "def _new_id() -> str:\n    return str(uuid.uuid4())\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- work items\n\n\nasync def create_work_item(db: AsyncSession, payload: WorkItemCreate) -> WorkItemRecord:\n    if payload.parent_id is not None:\n        parent = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, payload.parent_id)\n        if parent is None:\n            raise ValueError(f\"parent work item {payload.parent_id} does not exist\")\n    if payload.project_id is not None:\n        from .a2a_models import ProjectRecord\n\n        project = await db.get(ProjectRecord, payload.project_id)\n        if project is None:\n            raise ValueError(f\"project {payload.project_id} does not exist\")\n    now = _now()\n    seq, work_uid = await allocate_work_uid(db, now)\n    record = WorkItemRecord(\n        work_item_id=_new_id(),\n        work_key=f\"ACMS-WORK-{seq:06d}\",\n        work_uid=work_uid,\n        work_sequence=seq,\n        parent_id=payload.parent_id,\n        kind=payload.kind.value,\n        title=payload.title,\n        created_by=payload.created_by,\n        project_id=payload.project_id,\n        status=WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value,\n        scope_markdown=payload.scope_markdown,\n        created_at=now,\n        updated_at=now,\n    )\n    db.add(record)\n    await db.commit()\n    await db.refresh(record)\n    return record", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# smoke-test.sh — ACMS post-deployment smoke checks (plan §11/§14).\n#\n# Usage: deploy/smoke-test.sh [base-url]\n#   default base URL: https://10.0.20.122 (the ACMS VM's own IP; the nginx\n#   allowlist admits the VM's source IP, and MARION has no home.arpa DNS —\n#   raw IPs are the convention).\n#\n# Checks backend (direct, IN-CONTAINER — the app publishes no host ports by\n# design; a previous version probed 127.0.0.1:8000 from the VM host, which\n# can never pass), HTTPS UI through the proxy, auth denial, build identity,\n# Work UI (Slice 1), and Docker health status.\n#\n# Exit code: 0 iff every check passed (a previous version counted failures\n# but always exited 0 — fixed).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nACMS_ENV_FILE=\"${ACMS_ENV_FILE:-/opt/acms/.env}\"\nBASE_URL=\"${1:-https://10.0.20.122}\"\nFAILURES=0\n\nCY=(docker compose -f \"$SCRIPT_DIR/compose.yaml\" --env-file \"$ACMS_ENV_FILE\")\n\ncheck() { # name, condition-command\n  local name=\"$1\"; shift\n  if \"$@\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    echo \"  [ok]   $name\"\n  else\n    echo \"  [FAIL] $name\"\n    FAILURES=$((FAILURES + 1))\n  fi\n}\n\n# probe <path> — GET http://127.0.0.1:8000<path> from inside the app\n# container (urllib; the image ships no curl). Prints the HTTP status.\nprobe() {\n  \"${CY[@]}\" exec -T acms-app python - \"$1\" <<'PY' 2>/dev/null\nimport sys, urllib.request, urllib.error\ntry:\n    r = urllib.request.urlopen(\"http://127.0.0.1:8000\" + sys.argv[1], timeout=5)\n    print(r.status)\nexcept urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n    print(e.code)\nexcept Exception:\n    print(\"ERR\")\nPY\n}\n\n# probe_ok <path> <expected-status> — exit 0 iff status matches exactly.\nprobe_ok() {\n  [ \"$(probe \"$1\")\" = \"$2\" ]\n}\n\necho \"==> Backend (direct, in-container — no host ports by design)\"\ncheck \"/health responds (200)\"                 probe_ok /health 200\ncheck \"/version reports build identity (200)\"  probe_ok /version 200\ncheck \"unauthenticated registry denied (401)\"  probe_ok /api/v1/agents 401", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "def test_home_and_system_pages_show_real_data_only(monkeypatch, ui_env):\n    resp = _login(monkeypatch, ADMIN_DN)\n    home = client.get(\"/ui/\", cookies=resp)\n    assert home.status_code == 200\n    # STEA-004 §29: agent status / last contact are REAL now (heartbeat poller);\n    # only cost remains honestly unimplemented (usage pipeline is separate).\n    assert \"Not yet implemented\" in home.text\n    assert \"primary assignment\" not in home.text\n    assert \"agent status\" not in home.text\n    assert \"last contact\" not in home.text\n    assert \"cost\" in home.text\n    # fleet cards render real values (never fabricated)\n    assert \"Needs human\" in home.text\n    assert \"Agents\" in home.text\n\n    system = client.get(\"/ui/system\", cookies=resp)\n    assert system.status_code == 200\n    assert \"development\" in system.text  # ACMS_ENVIRONMENT surface\n\n\ndef test_agents_page_lists_registered_rows(monkeypatch, ui_env):\n    reg = client.post(\n        \"/api/v1/agents/register\",\n        headers=AUTH,\n        json={\n            \"external_registration_id\": \"ui-test-agent-01\",\n            \"display_name\": \"UI Test Agent\",\n            \"trust_class\": \"internal\",\n            \"harness\": \"hermes\",\n            \"bridge_version\": \"0.1\",\n            \"protocol_version\": \"a2a\",\n            \"capabilities\": {\"streaming\": True, \"steering\": True},\n        },\n    )\n    assert reg.status_code == 201\n    resp = _login(monkeypatch, OBSERVER_DN)\n    page = client.get(\"/ui/agents\", cookies=resp)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"UI Test Agent\" in page.text\n    assert \"ui-test-agent-01\" not in page.text  # external_registration_id is not a UI field\n\n\ndef test_bearer_token_never_exposed_in_ui(monkeypatch, ui_env):\n    \"\"\"Machine bearer token must never appear in any UI response (plan §6).\"\"\"\n    resp = _login(monkeypatch, ADMIN_DN)\n    for path in (\"/ui/\", \"/ui/agents\", \"/ui/system\", \"/ui/login\"):\n        body = client.get(path, cookies=resp).text\n        assert \"test-token\" not in body\n        assert \"Bearer\" not in body\n\n\ndef test_ui_static_assets_served():\n    css = client.get(\"/ui/static/style.css\")\n    assert css.status_code == 200\n    assert \"text/css\" in css.headers[\"content-type\"]", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "\"\"\"Signed server-side session cookies for human web login (plan §6).\n\nToken format: ``base64url(json payload) + \".\" + base64url(HMAC-SHA256(secret, payload))``.\nPayload: ``{\"u\": username, \"r\": role, \"exp\": unix_epoch}``.\n\n- HttpOnly + SameSite=Lax always; ``Secure`` when configured (default on).\n- Fail closed: with no/placeholder ``ACMS_SESSION_SECRET`` the UI login is\n  disabled entirely rather than operating insecurely.\n- The machine bearer token is never used here or exposed to the browser.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport base64\nimport hashlib\nimport hmac\nimport json\nimport time\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status\n\nfrom ..settings import get_settings\n\nCOOKIE_NAME = \"acms_session\"\n\n_PLACEHOLDER_SECRETS = {\"\", \"change-me\", \"replace-with-a-random-secret\"}\n\n\nclass SessionUser:\n    __slots__ = (\"username\", \"role\", \"expires_at\")\n\n    def __init__(self, username: str, role: str, expires_at: int) -> None:\n        self.username = username\n        self.role = role\n        self.expires_at = expires_at\n\n\ndef _b64encode(data: bytes) -> str:\n    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(data).rstrip(b\"=\").decode(\"ascii\")\n\n\ndef _b64decode(data: str) -> bytes:\n    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(data + \"=\" * (-len(data) % 4))\n\n\ndef _sign(secret: str, payload_b64: str) -> str:\n    digest = hmac.new(secret.encode(), payload_b64.encode(), hashlib.sha256).digest()\n    return _b64encode(digest)\n\n\ndef session_configured() -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when a real session signing secret is configured.\"\"\"\n    return get_settings().session_secret not in _PLACEHOLDER_SECRETS\n\n\ndef issue_token(username: str, role: str, lifetime_minutes: int | None = None) -> str:\n    settings = get_settings()\n    if not session_configured():\n        raise RuntimeError(\"ACMS_SESSION_SECRET is not configured; UI login is disabled\")\n    minutes = lifetime_minutes if lifetime_minutes is not None else settings.session_lifetime_minutes\n    payload = json.dumps(\n        {\"u\": username, \"r\": role, \"exp\": int(time.time()) + minutes * 60},\n        separators=(\",\", \":\"),\n    )\n    payload_b64 = _b64encode(payload.encode())\n    return f\"{payload_b64}.{_sign(settings.session_secret, payload_b64)}\"\n\n\ndef verify_token(token: str | None) -> SessionUser | None:\n    if not token or \".\" not in token or not session_configured():\n        return None\n    payload_b64, sig = token.split(\".\", 1)\n    settings = get_settings()\n    if not hmac.compare_digest(sig, _sign(settings.session_secret, payload_b64)):\n        return None\n    try:\n        payload = json.loads(_b64decode(payload_b64))\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n    if not isinstance(payload, dict):\n        return None\n    try:\n        expires_at = int(payload.get(\"exp\", 0))\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return None\n    if expires_at < time.time():\n        return None\n    username, role = payload.get(\"u\"), payload.get(\"r\")\n    if not isinstance(username, str) or not username or not isinstance(role, str) or not role:\n        return None\n    return SessionUser(username, role, expires_at)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> SessionUser:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: resolve the session cookie, else redirect to login.\"\"\"\n    user = verify_token(request.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME))\n    if user is None:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER,\n            headers={\"Location\": \"/ui/login\"},\n        )\n    return user\n\n\ndef set_session_cookie(response, token: str, secure: bool) -> None:\n    response.set_cookie(\n        COOKIE_NAME,\n        token,\n        max_age=get_settings().session_lifetime_minutes * 60,\n        httponly=True,\n        secure=secure,\n        samesite=\"lax\",\n        path=\"/\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response, secure: bool) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(\n        COOKIE_NAME,\n        path=\"/\",\n        secure=secure,\n        httponly=True,\n        samesite=\"lax\",\n    )\n1:\"\"\"Human access roles (ACMS-REQ-023) and LLDAP group mapping.\n3:LLDAP groups map to ACMS roles via configured group DNs. Group membership is\n27:def highest_role(member_of_dns: list[str]) -> Role | None:\n28:    \"\"\"Map LLDAP group DNs to the user's highest ACMS role.\n31:    in several mapped groups, the highest-privilege role wins so behavior is\n42:    for group_dn, role in configured.items():\n46:            if best is None or _ROLE_RANK[role] > _ROLE_RANK[best]:\n47:                best = role\n52:# role (plan §6: Administrator all pages, Worker read views, Observer read-only).", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

**Phase C 架构计划：**
1. **`acms/ui/operator_data.py`** — 为首页区域和看板共享数据加载器（单一事实来源）。
2. **首页 (C1)** — 区域：Work Now、Agent Fleet、Human Attention、System/Cost Health、Product Slop 卡片。
3. **看板 (C2)** — 计划 §12 列，包括"Human Attention/Blocked"并支持通过 UID 跳转到详情。
4. **工作详情 (C3)** — 突出显示规范移交卡片。
5. **代理聊天 (C4)** — 新增 `/ui/agents/{id}/chat` 页面 + 后端路由 (通过 bridge 进行的消息、历史记录、支持 harness 自动补全的斜杠命令)。
6. **收件箱 (C5)** — 上一条/下一条导航。
7. **产品/项目 (C6)** — 带有存储库 + Slop 卡片的详情页。
8. **Artifacts (C7)** — 可排序/可搜索列表。
9. **使用情况 (C8)** — 设施电力卡片 + 使用情况拆解。
10. **搜索栏 (C9) + CSS** — base.html 和 style.css。

[Subdirectory context discovered: .hermes/hermes-agent/AGENTS.md]
# Hermes Agent - Development Guide

Instructions for AI coding assistants and developers working on the hermes-agent codebase.

**Never give up on the right solution.**

## What Hermes Is

Hermes is a personal AI agent that runs the same agent core across a CLI, a
messaging gateway (Telegram, Discord, Slack, and ~20 other platforms), a TUI,
and an Electron desktop app. It learns across sessions (memory + skills),
delegates to subagents, runs scheduled jobs, and drives a real terminal and
browser. It is extended primarily through **plugins and skills**, not by
growing the core.

Two properties shape almost every design decision and are the lens for
reviewing any change:

- **Per-conversation prompt caching is sacred.** A long-lived conversation
  reuses a cached prefix every turn. Anything that mutates past context,
  swaps toolsets, or rebuilds the system prompt mid-conversation invalidates
  that cache and multiplies the user's cost. We do not do it (the one
  exception is context compression).
- **The core is a narrow waist; capability lives at the edges.** Every model
  tool we add is sent on every API call, so the bar for a new *core* tool is
  high. Most new capability should arrive as a CLI command + skill, a
  service-gated tool, or a plugin — not as core surface.

## Contribution Rubric — What We Want / What We Don't

This is the project's intent layer. Use it two ways:

1. **For humans and for your own work** — what gets merged and what gets
   rejected, so a contribution aims at the target.
2. **For automated review (the triage sweeper)** — guidance on when a PR is
   safe to close on the three allowed reasons (`implemented_on_main`,
   `cannot_reproduce`, `incoherent`) and, just as important, **when NOT to
   close** one. Taste-based "we don't want this / out of scope" closes are NOT
   an automated decision — those stay with a human maintainer. The sweeper's
   job here is to recognize design intent and *avoid wrongly closing a
   legitimate contribution*, not to make the won't-implement call itself.

Read the balance right: Hermes ships a **lot** — most merges are bug fixes to
real reported behavior, and the product surface (platforms, channels,
providers, models, desktop/TUI features) expands aggressively and on purpose.
The restraint below is aimed squarely at the **core agent + the model tool
schema**, the one place where every addition is paid for on every API call.
"Smallest footprint" governs *how a capability is wired into the core*, NOT
whether the product is allowed to grow. We are expansive at the edges and
conservative at the waist.

### What we want

- **Fix real bugs, well.** The bulk of what lands is `fix(...)` against an
  actual reported symptom. A good fix reproduces the symptom on current
  `main`, points to the exact line where it manifests, and fixes the whole bug
  class — sibling call paths included — not just the one site the reporter hit.
- **Expand reach at the edges.** New platform adapters, channels, providers,
  models, and desktop/TUI/dashboard features are welcome and land routinely,
  including large ones (a new messaging channel, a session-cap feature, a
  Windows PTY bridge). Breadth in the product is a goal, not a footprint
  concern — as long as it integrates with the existing setup/config UX
  (`hermes tools`, `hermes setup`, auto-install) rather than bolting on a raw
  env var.
- **Refactor god-files into clean modules.** Extracting a multi-thousand-line
  cluster out of `cli.py` / `run_agent.py` / `gateway/run.py` into a focused
  mixin or module is wanted work, even when the diff is huge and mechanical
  (large `+N/-N` refactors merge regularly). The "every line traces to the
  request" test applies to *feature* PRs; a declared refactor's request IS the
  extraction.
- **Keep the core narrow.** New *model tools* are the expensive exception —
  every tool ships on every API call. Prefer, in order: extend existing code →
  CLI command + skill → service-gated tool (`check_fn`) → plugin → MCP server
  in the catalog → new core tool (last resort). See "The Footprint Ladder."
- **Extend, don't duplicate.** Before adding a module/manager/hook, check
  whether existing infrastructure already covers the use case. When several PRs
  integrate the same *category*, design one shared interface instead of merging
  them one at a time (see the ABC + orchestrator note under the Footprint
  Ladder).
- **Behavior contracts over snapshots.** Tests should assert how two pieces of
  data must relate (invariants), not freeze a current value (model lists,
  config version literals, enumeration counts). See "Don't write
  change-detector tests."
- **E2E validation, not just green unit mocks.** For anything touching
  resolution chains, config propagation, security boundaries, remote
  backends, or file/network I/O, exercise the real path with real imports
  against a temp `HERMES_HOME`. Mocks hide integration bugs.
- **Cache-, alternation-, and invariant-safe.** Preserve prompt caching, strict
  message role alternation (never two same-role messages in a row; never a
  synthetic user message injected mid-loop), and a system prompt that is
  byte-stable for the life of a conversation.
- **Contributor credit preserved.** Salvage external work by cherry-picking
  (rebase-merge) so authorship survives in git history; don't reimplement from
  scratch when you can build on top.

### What we don't want (rejected even when well-built)

- **Speculative infrastructure.** Hooks, callbacks, or extension points with no
  concrete consumer. Adding a hook is easy; removing one after plugins depend
  on it is hard. A hook is NOT speculative if a contributor has a real, stated
  use case — even if the consumer ships separately.
- **New `HERMES_*` env vars for non-secret config.** `.env` is for secrets
  only (API keys, tokens, passwords). All behavioral settings — timeouts,
  thresholds, feature flags, display prefs — go in `config.yaml`. Bridge to an
  internal env var if the mechanism needs one, but user-facing docs point to
  `config.yaml`. Reject PRs that tell users to "set X in your .env" unless X
  is a credential.
- **A new core tool when terminal + file already do the job, or when a skill
  would.** If the only barrier is file visibility on a remote backend, fix the
  mount, not the toolset.
- **Lazy-reading escape hatches on instructional tools.** No `offset`/`limit`
  pagination on tools that load content the agent must read fully (skills,
  prompts, playbooks). Models will read page 1 and skip the rest.
- **"Fixes" that destroy the feature they secure.** A mitigation that kills the
  feature's purpose is the wrong mitigation. Read the original commit's intent
  (`git log -p -S`) before restricting behavior; find a fix that preserves the
  feature.
- **Outbound telemetry / usage attribution without opt-in gating.** No new
  analytics, third-party identifier tagging, or attribution tags until a
  generic user-facing opt-in (config gate + setup prompt + `hermes tools`
  toggle) exists. Park behind a label, do not merge.
- **Change-detector tests, cache-breaking mid-conversation, dead code wired in
  without E2E proof, and plugins that touch core files.** Plugins live in their
  own directory and work within the ABCs/hooks we provide; if a plugin needs
  more, widen the generic plugin surface, don't special-case it in core.
- **Third-party products / other people's projects integrated into the core
  tree.** Observability backends, vendor SaaS integrations, analytics dashboards,
  and similar "someone else's product" plugins do NOT land under `plugins/` in
  this repo. They place an ongoing maintenance burden on us to keep them working
  against a fast-moving core, for a backend we don't own. Ship them as a
  **standalone plugin repo** users install into `~/.hermes/plugins/` (or via a
  pip entry point), and promote them in the Nous Research Discord
  (`#plugins-skills-and-skins`). This is a coupling-and-maintenance decision, not

see\n# docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md for the test log).\nHERMES_SUPPORTED_CAPABILITIES = (\n    \"status\", \"send_work\", \"steer\", \"interrupt\", \"cancel\",\n    \"request_handoff\", \"set_session_title\",\n)\n\n\nclass BridgeError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Bridge transport/protocol failure (maps to reconciliation failure).\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _post_json(url: str, key: str, body: dict | None = None, method: str = \"POST\",\n               headers: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 20) -> dict:\n    data = json.dumps(body or {}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)\n    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n    for k, v in (headers or {}).items():\n        req.add_header(k, v)\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n        detail = e.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")[:300]\n        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n\n\ndef _get_json(base_url: str, key: str, path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{base_url}{path}\")\n    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"output": "class CostAttributionRecord(Base):\n    \"\"\"Per-execution cost correlated to a PR outcome (REV2 §9B.4).\n\n    ``failed_run`` rows keep their cost in every rollup — failed attempts are\n    part of cost-per-accepted, never divided away. Local USD-equivalent stays\n    nullable until an authoritative source exists (FW-LLM-LOCAL-COST).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    __tablename__ = \"cost_attribution\"\n\n    entry_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)\n    outcome_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), index=True)\n    session_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), index=True, nullable=True)\n    model_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)\n    provider: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)\n    input_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)\n    output_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)\n    cached_input_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)\n    api_cost_usd: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)\n    local_compute_seconds: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)\n    local_cost_usd: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)\n    # session | aggregate | manual\n    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), default=\"session\")\n    failed_run: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(default=False)\n    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=_now)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def new_id() -> str:", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

### [2026-10-02T02:40:49.097154+00:00] assistant

**发现实时 Bug**：`ingest_power_snapshot` 持久化了 `payload_json` 时**丢弃了 channels**（仅包含 error/totals/collector_health），但 `latest_power_summary`（UI 读取的数据源）却从 `payload_json` 中重构了 channels。刷新响应直接根据实时获取的数据构建，因此它包含 channels，但存储的快照（UI 显示的内容）却为空。我来修复 `power_ingest.py` 以持久化 channels：

---

### [2026-10-02T02:40:49.125423+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "94|async def ingest_power_snapshot(db: AsyncSession) -> dict:\n95|    \"\"\"Fetch + store one snapshot. Returns the summary the API responds with.\"\"\"\n96|    client = FacilityPowerClient()\n97|    payload = client.fetch()\n98|    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n99|    totals = payload.get(\"totals\") or {}\n100|    collector = payload.get(\"collector\") or {}\n101|    channels = payload.get(\"channels\") or []\n102|    current_watts = None\n103|    last_ts = None\n104|    try:\n105|        watts = [c.get(\"avg_watts_1h\") for c in channels]\n106|        if watts and not any(c.get(\"stale\") for c in channels):\n107|            current_watts = round(sum(float(w or 0) for w in watts), 1)\n108|        tses = [c.get(\"last_sample_ts\") for c in channels if c.get(\"last_sample_ts\")]\n109|        if tses:\n110|            last_ts = max(datetime.fromisoformat(t) for t in tses)\n111|    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — parse failures never crash ingest\n112|        pass\n113|    rec = PowerSnapshotRecord(\n114|        snapshot_id=PowerSnapshotRecord.new_id(),\n115|        captured_at=now, source=\"llm-manager-facility\",\n116|        payload_json=json.dumps({\"error\": payload.get(\"error\"), \"totals\": totals,\n117|                                 \"collector_health\": bool(collector.get(\"healthy\"))})[:500],\n118|        total_kwh_24h=totals.get(\"kwh_24h\"),\n119|        total_cost_usd_24h=totals.get(\"cost_usd_24h\"),\n120|        total_kwh_30d=totals.get(\"kwh_30d\"),\n121|        total_cost_usd_30d=totals.get(\"cost_usd_30d\"),\n122|        current_watts=current_watts,\n123|        collector_stale=bool(collector.get(\"stale\")) if collector else None,\n124|        last_sample_ts=last_ts,\n125|    )\n126|    db.add(rec)\n127|    await db.commit()\n128|    return _summary_from(payload, rec)\n129|\n130|\n131|def _summary_from(payload: dict, rec: PowerSnapshotRecord) -> dict:\n132|    \"\"\"Shape the API response (§17): stale → STALE + timestamp + NULL, never 0.\"\"\"\n133|    channels = payload.get(\"channels\") or []\n134|", "total_lines": 234, "file_size": 10453, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=134 to continue reading (showing 94-133 of 234 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

expected),\n                                \"in_model_not_migration\": sorted(expected - actual),\n                            })\n                await conn.run_sync(_sync_inspect)\n        finally:\n            await eng.dispose()\n        return drifts\n\n    drifts = asyncio.run(_collect())\n    assert drifts == [], (\n        \"Migration chain vs ORM model drift detected (the 0008 commit_shas class): \"", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

### [2026-10-02T02:54:27.773798+00:00] assistant

这是我的 `standalone` probe 的问题（缺少模型导入），而非应用本身的问题。演示的 archive-refused 检查失败了，因为演示的 `ACTION_REQUIRED` 项已经... 等等，之前的演示（在修复消息之前）已经强制归档了 `demo-phasec-ar`。新项 `demo-phasec-ar-r2`——重定向检查需要该表单确实返回了 `archive_warn`。让我追踪一下发生了什么：archive action 表单处理程序是 `/ui/inbox/{item_id}/action`，带有 `action=archive`。我的 demo 对 `redirects` 进行了 `urlopen`——带有 303 的 `urllib.request.urlopen` 会**跟随重定向**，因此 `resp` 是最终的 200 页面，而 `resp.headers.get('location')` 会是 `None`。这就是 bug 出现的地方——我需要在不进行重定向的情况下进行探测。此外，“archive warning page renders” 也因为同样的原因而失败（它随后渲染了归档成功重定向到的列表页）。通过一个 `no-redirect opener` 来修复该演示：

---

### [2026-10-02T02:54:27.814344+00:00] tool · tool: patch

{"success": true, "name": "telegram-send-file", "description": "Deliver a local file as a Telegram document to the user's DM when no send_message/file-delivery tool is available", "tags": [], "related_skills": [], "content": "---\nname: telegram-send-file\ndescription: Deliver a local file as a Telegram document to the user's DM when no send_message/file-delivery tool is available\ntrigger: User asks to send/deliver a file \"over Telegram\" / \"to Telegram here\" and the active toolset lacks a send_message or file-delivery tool\n---\n\n# Sending a file via Telegram Bot API\n\n## When\nHermes gateway is connected to Telegram (check `gateway_state.json` → `platforms.telegram.state: connected`) but no `send_message` tool exists in the toolset. Use the Bot API directly.\n\n## Steps\n\n1. **Verify the file exists + secret-scan it** before sending:\n   ```bash\n   ls -la <file> && grep -nEi 'sk-[A-Za-z0-9]|ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]|gho_[A-Za-z0-9]|xox[bap]-|Bearer [A-Za-z0-9]|password|vck_|vcp_|GOCSPX' <file> | head -20\n   ```\n   Abort on hits; never deliver secrets to a cloud API.\n\n2. **Get the token WITHOUT printing it.** Simplest source, verified 2026-10-01: the profile `.env`\n   carries `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` and `TELEGRAM_HOME_CHANNEL` directly:\n   ```python\n   env = {}\n   for line in open(\"/home/jordatech/.hermes/profiles/<profile>/.env\"):\n       line = line.strip()\n       if line and not line.startswith(\"#\") and \"=\" in line:\n           k, v = line.split(\"=\", 1)\n           env[k] = v\n   tok, chat_id = env[\"TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN\"], env[\"TELEGRAM_HOME_CHANNEL\"]\n   ```\n   Fallback if the profile `.env` lacks it: `hermes_cli.config.get_env_value('TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN')`\n   with `sys.path.insert(0, '/home/jordatech/.hermes/hermes-agent')` (credential store resolves it;\n   `print('present:', bool(tok), 'len:', len(tok))` — never the value).\n\n4. **Upload via multipart POST** (stdlib only, token never echoed):\n   ```python\n   import sys, json, urllib.request, urllib.error, uuid\n   sys.path.insert(0, '/home/jordatech/.hermes/hermes-agent')\n   from hermes_cli.config import get_env_value\n   tok = get_env_value('TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN')\n   chat_id = \"<chat_id>\"; path = \"<file>\"\n   boundary = uuid.uuid4().hex\n   def part_field(name, value):\n       return (f\"--{boundary}\\r\\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\\\"{name}\\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n{value}\\r\\n\").encode()\n   def part_file(name, filename, content):\n       return (f\"--{boundary}\\r\\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\\\"{name}\\\"; filename=\\\"{filename}\\\"\\r\\n\"\n               f\"Content-Type: text/markdown\\r\\n\\r\\n\").encode() + content + b\"\\r\\n\"\n   body  = part_field(\"chat_id\", chat_id)\n   body += part_field(\"caption\", \"<short caption>\")\n   body += part_file(\"document\", path.split('/')[-1], open(path, \"rb\").read())\n   body += f\"--{boundary}--\\r\\n\".encode()\n   req = urllib.request.Request(f\"https://api.telegram.org/bot{tok}/sendDocument\",\n       data=body, headers={\"Content-Type\": f\"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}\"}, method=\"POST\")\n   with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r:\n       resp = json.load(r)\n   print(\"ok:\", resp.get(\"ok\"), \"message_id:\", resp.get(\"result\", {}).get(\"message_id\"),\n         \"file_size:\", resp.get(\"result\", {}).get(\"document\", {}).get(\"file_size\"))\n   ```\n\n5. **Verify delivery**: confirm `ok: True` and that `file_size` matches the source byte count. Report message_id as proof.\n\n## Pitfalls\n- Use `sendDocument` (not `sendPhoto`) for `.md`/text files — preserves the file as a downloadable document.\n- 50 MB upload limit per file; larger files need chunking or an external link.\n- The gateway process env has NO telegram vars (`/proc/<pid>/environ` is clean) — the token lives in Hermes' credential layer, only reachable via `get_env_value`.\n- `caption` max 1024 chars; keep it a summary, put details in the file.\n- As a final fallback, media can also be referenced with the MEDIA:/file-delivery convention in a normal reply if the platform supports native attachments — but this Bot API path works even when that doesn't render.\n", "path": "messaging/telegram-send-file/SKILL.md", "skill_dir": "/home/jordatech/.hermes/profiles/agent_stea004_entrepreneur/skills/messaging/telegram-send-file", "linked_files": null, "usage_hint": null, "required_environment_variables": [], "required_commands": [], "missing_required_environment_variables": [], "missing_credential_files": [], "missing_required_commands": [], "setup_needed": false, "setup_skipped": false, "readiness_status": "available"}
